   196	# ───────────────────────── develop-tip resolution ───────────────────────
   197	
   198	
   199	def _gerrit_query_argv(
   200	    *, host: str, port: int, key_path: Path | None, query: str
   201	) -> list[str]:
   202	    argv = ["ssh"]
   203	    if key_path is not None:
   204	        argv += ["-i", str(key_path)]
   205	    argv += [
   206	        "-p", str(port),
   207	        "-o", "BatchMode=yes",
   208	        "-o", "StrictHostKeyChecking=accept-new",
   209	        host,
   210	        "gerrit", "query",
   211	        "--format=JSON",
   212	        # Required on Gerrit 3.13: ``gerrit query`` no longer emits the
   213	        # ``currentPatchSet`` block by default (see OP-959 and
   214	        # ``release_milestone_checker.SshGerritClient._query``).
   215	        "--current-patch-set",
   216	        query,
   217	    ]
   218	    return argv
   219	
   220	
   221	def resolve_develop_tip(
   222	    *,
   223	    runner: Runner,
   224	    host: str,
   225	    port: int,
   226	    key_path: Path | None,
   227	    project: str,
   228	    timeout: int = 30,
   229	) -> str:
   230	    """Return the develop-tip commit SHA Gerrit currently has merged.
   231	
   232	    Mirrors ``release_milestone_checker.SshGerritClient.develop_tip`` so the
   233	    ``revision`` we stamp into the JSONL line matches the one the checker
   234	    queries for that gate. Raises ``RuntimeError`` on any failure — the
   235	    caller turns that into exit code 3 (we cannot honestly emit a gate
   236	    status without knowing which revision it is for).
   237	    """
   238	    argv = _gerrit_query_argv(
   239	        host=host, port=port, key_path=key_path,
   240	        query=f"project:{project} branch:{BRANCH} status:merged",
   241	    )
   242	    proc = runner(argv, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, check=True)
   243	    for raw in (proc.stdout or "").splitlines():
   244	        try:
   245	            row = json.loads(raw)
   246	        except json.JSONDecodeError:
   247	            continue
   248	        if not isinstance(row, dict) or row.get("type") == "stats":
   249	            continue
   250	        revision = str((row.get("currentPatchSet") or {}).get("revision") or "")
   251	        if revision:
   252	            return revision
   253	    raise RuntimeError(
   254	        "Gerrit develop query returned no merged change with "
   255	        "currentPatchSet.revision (expected `gerrit query --current-patch-set`)"
   256	    )
   257	
   258

   260

   262	        return None
   263	    digest = image.get("digest")
   264	    return digest if isinstance(digest, str) and digest else None
   265	
   266	
   267	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   268	#  OP-1582 (RT-08) — deployment overlay env lock
   269	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   270	# The deploy/promote step writes a lock file (env-file format) carrying the
   271	# running deployment's identity: the build git sha/ref, the promoted image
   272	# tag, the backend+frontend digest PAIR (RT-21), and the promotion audit id.
   273	# Per ADR-0040 RT-08-pre the container reads this lock ONCE AT STARTUP; the
   274	# cached snapshot is served on ``/api/version`` and gates ``/readyz``. Read-
   275	# before-start is deliberate: deploys always rolling-restart, so "needs a
   276	# restart to update" is not a limitation, and it avoids per-request disk I/O
   277	# and cache-invalidation failure modes. Fail-closed — a deployed runtime that
   278	# starts without (or with an incomplete) lock must not advertise readiness, so
   279	# the deploy gate catches it (see ``backend.routers.health._check_deploy_overlay``).
   280	DEPLOY_OVERLAY_LOCK_PATH = Path(
   281	    os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_DEPLOY_OVERLAY_LOCK", "/etc/omnisight/deploy-overlay.lock")
   282	)
   283	
   284	# Env-lock variable name → ``/api/version`` overlay field name. The deploy
   285	# writer emits these as ``KEY=value`` lines (the same file the prod/staging
   286	# compose ``env_file:`` sources, hence "env lock"). Every field is required:
   287	# a partial lock means the writer is broken and we must not advertise a
   288	# half-known identity, so a missing/empty value fails the overlay closed.
   289	_OVERLAY_LOCK_FIELDS: dict[str, str] = {
   290	    "OMNISIGHT_BUILD_GIT_SHA": "build_git_sha",
   291	    "OMNISIGHT_BUILD_GIT_REF": "build_git_ref",
   292	    "OMNISIGHT_DEPLOYED_TAG": "deployed_tag",
   293	    "OMNISIGHT_DEPLOYED_DIGEST_BACKEND": "deployed_digest_backend",
   294	    "OMNISIGHT_DEPLOYED_DIGEST_FRONTEND": "deployed_digest_frontend",
   295	    "OMNISIGHT_PROMOTION_AUDIT_ID": "promotion_audit_id",
   296	}
   297	
   298	#: The overlay payload fields, in serve order. Always present on
   299	#: ``/api/version`` (as ``None`` when no valid lock was read) so the contract
   300	#: shape is stable across dev and deployed images.
   301	OVERLAY_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(_OVERLAY_LOCK_FIELDS.values())
   302	
   303	# Sentinel for "not yet loaded" so the cache can distinguish that from
   304	# "loaded, but no valid lock present" (``None``).
   305	_OVERLAY_UNSET = object()
   306	_deploy_overlay_cache: object = _OVERLAY_UNSET
   307	
   308	
   309	def _parse_env_lock(text: str) -> dict[str, str]:
   310	    """Parse an env-file (``KEY=value``) lock into a plain dict.
   311	
   312	    Tolerant of blank lines, ``#`` comments, an optional ``export`` prefix,
   313	    and single/double-quoted values — the shapes a shell-written deploy lock
   314	    or a compose ``env_file:`` can take. Unknown keys are kept; the caller
   315	    selects the ones it cares about.
   316	    """
   317	    out: dict[str, str] = {}
   318	    for raw in text.splitlines():
   319	        line = raw.strip()
   320	        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
   321	            continue
   322	        if line.startswith("export "):
   323	            line = line[len("export "):].lstrip()
   324	        if "=" not in line:
   325	            continue
   326	        key, _, value = line.partition("=")
   327	        key = key.strip()
   328	        value = value.strip()
   329	        if len(value) >= 2 and value[0] == value[-1] and value[0] in {'"', "'"}:
   330	            value = value[1:-1]
   331	        if key:
   332	            out[key] = value
   333	    return out
   334	
   335	
   336	def load_deploy_overlay(path: Path | None = None) -> dict | None:
   337	    """Read the deploy-written env lock and return the overlay, or ``None``.
   338	
   339	    Returns ``None`` (the fail-closed marker) when the lock is absent,
   340	    unreadable, or incomplete — i.e. any of the six required identity fields
   341	    is missing or empty. Otherwise returns a dict keyed by the
   342	    :data:`OVERLAY_FIELDS` names. Does NOT cache — :func:`init_deploy_overlay`
   343	    owns the read-once-at-startup cache.
   344	    """
   345	    if path is None:
   346	        path = DEPLOY_OVERLAY_LOCK_PATH
   347	    try:
   348	        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   349	    except FileNotFoundError:
   350	        return None
   351	    except OSError:
   352	        _log.warning("Failed to read deploy overlay lock at %s", path)
   353	        return None
   354	
   355	    env = _parse_env_lock(text)
   356	    overlay: dict = {}
   357	    for env_key, field in _OVERLAY_LOCK_FIELDS.items():
   358	        value = env.get(env_key, "").strip()
   359	        overlay[field] = value or None
   360	
   361	    if any(overlay.get(field) is None for field in OVERLAY_FIELDS):
   362	        missing = [f for f in OVERLAY_FIELDS if overlay.get(f) is None]
   363	        _log.warning(
   364	            "deploy overlay lock at %s is incomplete; missing %s",
   365	            path,
   366	            ",".join(missing),
   367	        )
   368	        return None
   369	    return overlay
   370	
   371	
   372	def init_deploy_overlay(path: Path | None = None) -> dict | None:
   373	    """Read the env lock ONCE and cache the snapshot (called at startup).
   374	
   375	    Subsequent reads go through :func:`get_deploy_overlay`, which returns the
   376	    cached snapshot rather than re-reading the file. Calling this again
   377	    refreshes the cache (used by tests via :func:`reset_deploy_overlay_cache`
   378	    + this function with an injected ``path``).
   379	    """
   380	    global _deploy_overlay_cache
   381	    _deploy_overlay_cache = load_deploy_overlay(path)
   382	    return _deploy_overlay_cache  # type: ignore[return-value]
   383	
   384	
   385	def get_deploy_overlay() -> dict | None:
   386	    """Return the cached deploy overlay, loading it on first access.
   387	
   388	    ``None`` means no valid lock was found at startup — the fail-closed
   389	    marker the ``/readyz`` overlay gate keys on.
   390	    """
   391	    if _deploy_overlay_cache is _OVERLAY_UNSET:
   392	        return init_deploy_overlay()
   393	    return _deploy_overlay_cache  # type: ignore[return-value]
   394	
   395	
   396	def reset_deploy_overlay_cache() -> None:
   397	    """Test helper — drop the cached overlay so the next access re-reads."""
   398	    global _deploy_overlay_cache
   399	    _deploy_overlay_cache = _OVERLAY_UNSET
   400	
   401	
   402	def build_version_payload(
   403	    *,
   404	    bundle_path: Path | None = None,
   405	    image_manifest_path: Path | None = None,
   406	    overlay: dict | None | object = _OVERLAY_UNSET,
   407	) -> dict:
   408	    """Assemble the JSON body returned by ``GET /api/version``.
   409	
   410	    Split out from the route handler so tests can drive it without
   411	    standing up a FastAPI app, and so callers can inject paths for
   412	    fixture-based testing. Defaults resolve against the module-level
   413	    constants at call time, so tests can monkeypatch ``av.BUNDLE_MANIFEST_PATH``
   414	    / ``av._IMAGE_MANIFEST_PATH`` and the route picks up the change.
   415	
   416	    ``overlay`` defaults to the cached deploy overlay snapshot
   417	    (:func:`get_deploy_overlay`); tests pass an explicit dict / ``None`` to
   418	    drive the RT-08 overlay fields without touching the cache.
   419	    """
   420	    if bundle_path is None:
   421	        bundle_path = BUNDLE_MANIFEST_PATH
   422	    if image_manifest_path is None:
   423	        image_manifest_path = _IMAGE_MANIFEST_PATH
   424	    if overlay is _OVERLAY_UNSET:
   425	        overlay = get_deploy_overlay()
   426	    bundle = _load_bundle_manifest(bundle_path)
   427	    warning: str | None = None
   428	    if bundle is None:
   429	        bundle = {}
   430	        warning = _MISSING_BUNDLE_WARNING

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""[OP-1643] Boreas-B A2 — dev/prod env-contract guard.
     2	
     3	Fail-closed when a process's declared environment (``OMNISIGHT_ENV``) does not
     4	match the environment its resolved Postgres DSN points at. This is the
     5	*env-declaration* layer that complements A1's *DB-credential* layer (the
     6	``omnisight_dev`` role is ``REVOKE``'d ``CONNECT`` on the prod ``omnisight`` DB):
     7	A1 catches a dev process by its credential; A2 catches a dev process that
     8	resolves a prod-looking DSN regardless of credential.
     9	
    10	Design + codex review: ``docs/sprint-boreas/boreas-b-A2-env-contract-guard-design.md``.
    11	
    12	This module is intentionally dependency-light (stdlib only) and must NOT import
    13	``backend.config`` / ``backend.db_url`` — it is called *from* the connection
    14	primitives (``db_pool.init_pool``, ``db._resolve_pg_dsn``,
    15	``db_url.DatabaseURL.asyncpg_connect_kwargs``, ``alembic/env.py``), so importing
    16	those back would create a cycle. It ships its own tolerant DSN parser.
    17	"""
    18	
    19	from __future__ import annotations
    20	
    21	import logging
    22	import os
    23	
    24	_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    25	
    26	# Exit 78 = EX_CONFIG (sysexits.h) — signals a config error to orchestrators,
    27	# matching backend.config.ConfigValidationError.
    28	_EXIT_CONFIG = 78
    29	
    30	# Known non-prod Postgres ports published on the co-tenanted host.
    31	_DEV_PORTS = {58432}
    32	_STAGING_PORTS = {55432, 55433}
    33	
    34	# Canonical env aliases (mirrors backend.config._resolve_yaml_path + the
    35	# startup validator, which only special-cased the literal "production").
    36	_ENV_ALIASES = {
    37	    "production": "prod",
    38	    "prod": "prod",
    39	    "develop": "dev",
    40	    "development": "dev",
    41	    "dev": "dev",
    42	    "staging": "staging",
    43	}
    44	
    45	
    46	class EnvContractViolation(SystemExit):
    47	    """Raised (exits 78) when env declaration and DB DSN disagree."""
    48	
    49	    def __init__(self, message: str) -> None:
    50	        super().__init__(_EXIT_CONFIG)
    51	        self.message = message
    52	        _logger.critical("EnvContractViolation: %s", message)
    53	
    54	
    55	def canonical_env(raw: str | None) -> str | None:
    56	    """Canonicalize ``OMNISIGHT_ENV`` → ``dev`` / ``staging`` / ``prod`` / None.
    57	
    58	    Unknown non-empty values are returned lower-cased as-is (so an unexpected
    59	    marker is treated as its own class and will fail the match, fail-closed).
    60	    """
    61	    if raw is None:
    62	        return None
    63	    v = raw.strip().lower()
    64	    if not v:
    65	        return None
    66	    return _ENV_ALIASES.get(v, v)
    67	
    68	
    69	def _split_dsn(dsn: str) -> tuple[str | None, str | None, int | None]:
    70	    """Tolerant parse → (user, db-name, port).
    71	
    72	    Avoids ``urllib.parse`` pitfalls with passwords containing ``/`` or ``@``:
    73	    the userinfo is everything up to the LAST ``@`` (libpq convention), and the
    74	    db-name is taken only from the path AFTER userinfo is stripped, so a ``/``
    75	    inside the password cannot be mistaken for the path separator.
    76	    """
    77	    from urllib.parse import unquote
    78	
    79	    rest = dsn.split("://", 1)[1] if "://" in dsn else dsn
    80	    at = rest.rfind("@")
    81	    userinfo = rest[:at] if at >= 0 else ""
    82	    authority = rest[at + 1:] if at >= 0 else rest
    83	
    84	    user = None
    85	    if userinfo:
    86	        user = unquote(userinfo.split(":", 1)[0]) or None
    87	
    88	    hostport, _, pathq = authority.partition("/")
    89	    db = pathq.split("?", 1)[0].strip("/")
    90	    db = unquote(db) if db else None
    91	
    92	    port: int | None = None
    93	    if ":" in hostport:
    94	        tail = hostport.rsplit(":", 1)[1]
    95	        if tail.isdigit():
    96	            port = int(tail)
    97	    return user, db, port
    98	
    99	
   100	def classify_dsn(dsn: str | None) -> str:
   101	    """Classify a DSN → ``"prod"`` / ``"dev"`` / ``"staging"`` / ``"sqlite"`` /
   102	    ``"skip"`` / ``"unknown"``.
   103	
   104	    - ``skip``    — empty, or a non-Postgres non-SQLite scheme (not our concern).
   105	    - ``sqlite``  — a SQLite URL (legacy / tests).
   106	    - ``unknown`` — a Postgres URL we cannot positively place (fail-closed: the
   107	      caller hard-fails on this, so a typo / renamed-prod / url-encoding bug
   108	      can't fail-open).
   109	    """
   110	    if not dsn or not dsn.strip():
   111	        return "skip"
   112	    s = dsn.strip()
   113	    scheme = s.split("://", 1)[0].lower() if "://" in s else s.lower()
   114	    if scheme.startswith("sqlite"):
   115	        return "sqlite"
   116	    if not scheme.startswith(("postgresql", "postgres", "asyncpg")):
   117	        return "skip"  # mysql/etc — not the omnisight PG we guard
   118	
   119	    user, db, port = _split_dsn(s)
   120	    db_l = (db or "").lower()
   121	    user_l = (user or "").lower()
   122	
   123	    if db_l.endswith(("_dev", "-dev")) or user_l == "omnisight_dev" or (port in _DEV_PORTS):
   124	        return "dev"
   125	    if db_l.endswith(("_staging", "-staging")) or (port in _STAGING_PORTS):
   126	        return "staging"
   127	    if db_l == "omnisight" and user_l == "omnisight":
   128	        return "prod"
   129	    return "unknown"
   130	
   131	
   132	def _truthy(val: str | None) -> bool:
   133	    return (val or "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")
   134	
   135	
   136	def enforce_env_db_contract(
   137	    dsn: str | None,
   138	    *,
   139	    source: str,
   140	    env: str | None = None,
   141	) -> None:
   142	    """Fail closed (exit 78) when ``OMNISIGHT_ENV`` disagrees with ``dsn``.
   143	
   144	    ``source`` is a short label for the call site (for the error/log message).
   145	    ``env`` overrides ``OMNISIGHT_ENV`` (used by tests). See the design doc for
   146	    the full truth table; summary:
   147	
   148	    ===============  ==========  =======
   149	    OMNISIGHT_ENV    DSN class   verdict
   150	    ===============  ==========  =======
   151	    dev              dev          OK
   152	    dev              prod/stg     FAIL  (core protection)
   153	    prod             prod         OK
   154	    prod             dev/stg      FAIL  (symmetric)
   155	    unset            prod         OK    (legacy prod-by-default: runners/coord)
   156	    unset            dev/stg      FAIL  (non-prod DB without declaring non-prod)
   157	    any              unknown-pg   FAIL  (fail-closed on unrecognised PG)
   158	    any              sqlite/skip  OK    (legacy/tests/non-PG)
   159	    ===============  ==========  =======
   160	    """
   161	    cls = classify_dsn(dsn)
   162	    if cls in ("sqlite", "skip"):
   163	        return
   164	
   165	    canon_env = canonical_env(env if env is not None else os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_ENV"))
   166	    redacted = _redact(dsn or "")
   167	
   168	    # Break-glass: refused in prod (env OR DSN) so it can never silence a real
   169	    # prod mismatch; valid prod states (unset+prod / prod+prod) never need it.
   170	    if _truthy(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_ENV_CONTRACT_DISABLE")):
   171	        if canon_env == "prod" or cls == "prod":
   172	            raise EnvContractViolation(
   173	                f"OMNISIGHT_ENV_CONTRACT_DISABLE=1 is refused when env or DSN is prod "
   174	                f"(env={canon_env!r}, dsn-class={cls!r}, source={source}, dsn={redacted})."
   175	            )
   176	        _logger.warning(
   177	            "env-contract DISABLED via OMNISIGHT_ENV_CONTRACT_DISABLE=1 "
   178	            "(env=%r, dsn-class=%r, source=%s) — break-glass, non-prod only.",
   179	            canon_env, cls, source,
   180	        )
   181	        return
   182	
   183	    # Test/CI carveout: tests call init_pool/connect directly with assorted
   184	    # DSNs. Bypass ONLY when not pointed at prod — a prod DSN inside a test is
   185	    # still a real violation worth surfacing.
   186	    in_test = bool(os.environ.get("PYTEST_CURRENT_TEST")) or _truthy(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_CI_MODE"))
   187	    if in_test and cls != "prod":
   188	        return
   189	
   190	    # Unrecognised Postgres → hard fail (never fail-open).
   191	    if cls == "unknown":
   192	        raise EnvContractViolation(
   193	            f"DSN resolves to an unrecognised Postgres database — refusing to connect "
   194	            f"(env={canon_env!r}, source={source}, dsn={redacted}). Expected a db/user "
   195	            f"matching dev (omnisight_dev), staging (*_staging) or prod (omnisight)."
   196	        )
   197	
   198	    # cls is one of dev/staging/prod from here.
   199	    expected = "prod" if canon_env is None else canon_env
   200	    if cls != expected:
   201	        env_desc = "unset (legacy prod-by-default)" if canon_env is None else repr(canon_env)
   202	        raise EnvContractViolation(
   203	            f"env-contract violation: OMNISIGHT_ENV={env_desc} but the DSN looks like "
   204	            f"'{cls}' (source={source}, dsn={redacted}). A '{expected}' process must use a "
   205	            f"'{expected}' database. Set OMNISIGHT_ENV to match, or point at the correct DB."
   206	        )
   207	
   208	
   209	def _redact(dsn: str) -> str:
   210	    """Mask the password in a DSN for safe logging."""
   211	    if "://" not in dsn or "@" not in dsn:
   212	        return dsn
   213	    scheme, rest = dsn.split("://", 1)
   214	    at = rest.rfind("@")
   215	    userinfo, tail = rest[:at], rest[at + 1:]
   216	    if ":" in userinfo:
   217	        userinfo = userinfo.split(":", 1)[0] + ":***"
   218	    return f"{scheme}://{userinfo}@{tail}"

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""OP-1590 -- RT-12 promote: retag validated GitLab CR digests to vX.Y.Z.
     3	
     4	Rewritten for the single-trunk release train (ADR-0040). A *promote*
     5	takes the backend+frontend image digests that were validated on staging
     6	(RT-05e) and migration-checked (RT-11) and retags them -- in GitLab CR,
     7	by digest, **never rebuilding** -- to the reserved release version
     8	``vX.Y.Z``. It then verifies each final tag resolves to the exact
     9	validated digest, writes a per-image promotion attestation, and appends
    10	exactly one bundle audit row (a hard gate: a failed audit write aborts
    11	the promote).
    12	
    13	Consumes the locked decisions (this ticket does NOT re-decide them):
    14	
    15	* **RT-20 -- final release identity is image-tag-only.** The release is
    16	  the GitLab CR image tag ``vX.Y.Z`` -> validated digest plus the
    17	  ``release_train`` / ``release_audit`` row. **No ``v*`` git tag is ever
    18	  created** -- a ``v*`` git tag would trip the existing ``^v`` CI build
    19	  rule and rebuild a *different* digest, breaking "validated digest ==
    20	  shipped digest". This script therefore only ever touches the registry
    21	  (``docker buildx imagetools``) and never invokes ``git`` or any image
    22	  build.
    23	* **RT-21 -- the train tracks the backend+frontend PAIR only.** ``bridge``
    24	  is a decoupled host control-plane daemon, not a prod container, so a
    25	  bundle that carries it (or omits either of the pair) is rejected.
    26	
    27	The retag is idempotent: a target tag that already resolves to the
    28	validated digest is left untouched, so re-running after a partial or
    29	failed promote is safe.
    30	"""
    31	from __future__ import annotations
    32	
    33	import argparse
    34	import json
    35	import os
    36	import re
    37	import subprocess
    38	import sys
    39	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    40	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    41	from pathlib import Path
    42	from typing import Any, Callable, Iterable, Sequence
    43	
    44	
    45	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    46	DEFAULT_AUDIT_LOG = REPO_ROOT / "audit" / "image_promotion_audit.jsonl"
    47	
    48	# RT-12: promotion targets GitLab CR (ADR-0038/0040), not GHCR. Mirrors
    49	# the ``OMNISIGHT_REGISTRY`` default the deploy compose files use so the
    50	# repository the promote retags is the one prod actually pulls.
    51	DEFAULT_REGISTRY = "sora.services:49154/omnisight/OmniSight-Productizer"
    52	
    53	SIGN_SCRIPT = REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "sign_promotion_attestation.py"
    54	VERIFY_SIGNATURE_SH = REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "verify_image_signature.sh"
    55	
    56	# RT-21: the release train is exactly this pair. Order is the per-image
    57	# attestation / audit order.
    58	REQUIRED_IMAGES: tuple[str, ...] = ("backend", "frontend")
    59	
    60	Runner = Callable[..., "subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]"]
    61	
    62	_DIGEST_RE = re.compile(r"^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$")
    63	# RT-20: the final tag is a release version, never an env alias.
    64	_VERSION_RE = re.compile(r"^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$")
    65	
    66	# Staging-gate (RT-05c/RT-05e) green signal as written by
    67	# ``backend.agents.staging_gate``.
    68	_STAGING_GREEN = "green"
    69	
    70	
    71	class PromoteError(RuntimeError):
    72	    """A promote precondition failed or a retag could not be verified."""
    73	
    74	
    75	class StagingGateNotPassed(PromoteError):
    76	    """No green staging-gate evidence matching the validated digests."""
    77

    79	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    80	class ImagePromotion:
    81	    name: str

    83	    digest: str
    84	    source_ref: str
    85	    target_ref: str

    87	
    88	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    89	class PromoteOutcome:
    90	    """Result of a promote: the version, the per-image plan, and whether
     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# scripts/check_deploy_ref.sh — gate which release identities may be
     3	# deployed to production.
     4	#
     5	# Release-train contract (RT-07a, ADR-0040): a production deploy
     6	# identity is ONLY one of:
     7	#   • a FINAL semver git tag  vX.Y.Z   (no -rc / -hotfix / pre-release
     8	#     suffix — every final tag is a promoted, prod-usable build)
     9	#   • an image DIGEST         sha256:<64 lowercase hex>
    10	# Branch deploys (main / develop / release/* / hotfix/* / any) are
    11	# REJECTED outright — there is no long-lived release branch under the
    12	# single-trunk release train.
    13	#
    14	# Layers (evaluated in order):
    15	#   Layer 0 — Final-ref shape gate (RT-07a): reject branch kinds, reject
    16	#             non-final tags, reject malformed digests. A digest is
    17	#             content-addressed and its trust is established by cosign
    18	#             verification at pull time (RT-06), so a well-formed digest
    19	#             needs no git allowlist / GPG check and accepts here.
    20	#   Layer 1 — Allowlist match (tags only): the tag must satisfy a rule
    21	#             in deploy/prod-deploy-allowlist.txt — the audit trail for
    22	#             "who said this ref may ship". (Allowlist file content is
    23	#             tightened separately in RT-07b.)
    24	#   Layer 2 — GPG signature (tags only): the annotated tag's signature
    25	#             must be made by a fingerprint in
    26	#             deploy/prod-deploy-signers.txt.
    27	#
    28	# Exit 0 on accept, non-zero on reject (stderr explains why).
    29	#
    30	# There is NO --insecure-skip-verify bypass (removed in RT-07a — it
    31	# bypassed both layers with no durable audit). The audited way to
    32	# authorise a new ref is a reviewed PR to the allowlist plus a signed
    33	# final tag, or deploying a cosign-verified image digest.
    34	#
    35	# Usage:
    36	#   scripts/check_deploy_ref.sh --kind tag    --ref v1.2.3
    37	#   scripts/check_deploy_ref.sh --kind digest --ref sha256:<64hex>
    38	#
    39	# Flags:
    40	#   --kind {tag,digest,branch}  (required) 'branch' is always rejected;
    41	#                               it is still accepted as an argument so
    42	#                               an accidental branch deploy gets a clear
    43	#                               release-train rejection rather than an
    44	#                               opaque arg error.
    45	#   --ref  <name>             (required)
    46	#   --allowlist-only          run Layer 0 (+ Layer 1 for tags) only;
    47	#                             used by the deploy --dry-run path and by
    48	#                             drift-guard tests. The ref does not need
    49	#                             to exist locally.
    50	#   --allowlist <path>        override allowlist file path
    51	#   --signers   <path>        override signers file path
    52	
    53	set -euo pipefail
    54	
    55	REPO="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
    56	ALLOWLIST="$REPO/deploy/prod-deploy-allowlist.txt"
    57	SIGNERS="$REPO/deploy/prod-deploy-signers.txt"
    58	KIND=""
    59	REF=""
    60	ALLOWLIST_ONLY=false
    61	
    62	# Final release tag: vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH only — no pre-release / build
    63	# suffix. A non-final tag (vX.Y.Z-rc.N, vX.Y.Z-hotfix.N, …) is NOT a
    64	# production deploy identity under the release train.
    65	FINAL_TAG_RE='^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$'
    66	# OCI image digest: sha256 + exactly 64 lowercase hex chars.
    67	DIGEST_RE='^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$'
    68	
    69	err() { echo "❌ check_deploy_ref: $*" >&2; exit 1; }
    70	warn() { echo "⚠️  check_deploy_ref: $*" >&2; }
    71	ok() { echo "✅ check_deploy_ref: $*" >&2; }
    72	
    73	while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
    74	    case "$1" in
    75	        --kind) KIND="${2:-}"; shift 2;;
    76	        --ref) REF="${2:-}"; shift 2;;
    77	        --allowlist-only) ALLOWLIST_ONLY=true; shift;;
    78	        --allowlist) ALLOWLIST="${2:-}"; shift 2;;
    79	        --signers) SIGNERS="${2:-}"; shift 2;;
    80	        -h|--help) sed -n '2,49p' "$0"; exit 0;;
    81	        *) err "unknown arg: $1";;
    82	    esac
    83	done
    84	
    85	[[ -n "$KIND" ]] || err "--kind is required (tag | digest | branch)"
    86	[[ -n "$REF" ]] || err "--ref is required"
    87	[[ "$KIND" == "branch" || "$KIND" == "tag" || "$KIND" == "digest" ]] \
    88	    || err "--kind must be 'tag', 'digest', or 'branch', got '$KIND'"
    89	
    90	# ── Layer 0: release-train final-ref shape gate (RT-07a) ─────────────
    91	# Decide acceptance purely on the shape of the requested identity. This
    92	# is the primary contract: branch → reject; non-final tag → reject;
    93	# malformed digest → reject; well-formed digest → accept (cosign owns
    94	# content-trust); final tag → continue to allowlist + GPG.
    95	case "$KIND" in
    96	    branch)
    97	        err "branch deploys are not permitted under the single-trunk release train (RT-07a). A production deploy identity is a FINAL tag (vX.Y.Z) or an image digest (sha256:<64hex>). Requested 'branch:$REF' rejected."
    98	        ;;
    99	    digest)
   100	        if [[ ! "$REF" =~ $DIGEST_RE ]]; then
   101	            err "digest '$REF' is malformed (expected sha256:<64 lowercase hex>)."
   102	        fi
   103	        ok "Layer 0: '$REF' is a well-formed image digest; content-trust is established by cosign verification at pull time (RT-06)"
   104	        exit 0
   105	        ;;
   106	    tag)
   107	        if [[ ! "$REF" =~ $FINAL_TAG_RE ]]; then
   108	            err "tag '$REF' is not a FINAL release tag (expected vX.Y.Z, with no -rc/-hotfix/pre-release suffix). Only promoted final tags may reach prod (RT-07a)."
   109	        fi
   110	        ok "Layer 0: '$REF' is a final release tag"
   111	        ;;
   112	esac
   113	
   114	# Below here KIND is always 'tag' (branch errored, digest exited).
   115	
   116	# ── Layer 1: allowlist ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   117	[[ -f "$ALLOWLIST" ]] || err "allowlist file missing: $ALLOWLIST"
   118	
   119	_match_allowlist() {
   120	    local kind="$1" ref="$2" file="$3"
   121	    local rule_kind rule_value line
   122	    while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
   123	        line="${line//$'\r'/}"
   124	        line="${line%%#*}"
   125	        # trim leading + trailing whitespace
   126	        line="${line#"${line%%[![:space:]]*}"}"
   127	        line="${line%"${line##*[![:space:]]}"}"
   128	        [[ -z "$line" ]] && continue
   129	        rule_kind="${line%% *}"
   130	        rule_value="${line#* }"
   131	        # trim again in case of multiple spaces
   132	        rule_value="${rule_value#"${rule_value%%[![:space:]]*}"}"
   133	        case "$rule_kind" in
   134	            branch)
   135	                [[ "$kind" == "branch" && "$ref" == "$rule_value" ]] && return 0
   136	                ;;
   137	            branch-regex)
   138	                if [[ "$kind" == "branch" ]] && [[ "$ref" =~ $rule_value ]]; then
   139	                    return 0
   140	                fi
   141	                ;;
   142	            tag-regex)
   143	                if [[ "$kind" == "tag" ]] && [[ "$ref" =~ $rule_value ]]; then
   144	                    return 0
   145	                fi
   146	                ;;
   147	            *)
   148	                err "allowlist syntax error in $file: unknown rule kind '$rule_kind' (expected branch | branch-regex | tag-regex)"
   149	                ;;
   150	        esac
   151	    done < "$file"
   152	    return 1
   153	}
   154	
   155	if ! _match_allowlist "$KIND" "$REF" "$ALLOWLIST"; then
   156	    err "ref 'tag:$REF' is NOT permitted by $ALLOWLIST. Add an explicit tag-regex rule via PR (audit trail)."
   157	fi
   158	ok "Layer 1: ref 'tag:$REF' matched allowlist"
   159	
   160	if [[ "$ALLOWLIST_ONLY" == "true" ]]; then
   161	    exit 0
   162	fi
   163	
   164	# ── Layer 2: GPG signature verification ─────────────────────────────
   165	[[ -f "$SIGNERS" ]] || err "signers file missing: $SIGNERS"
   166	
   167	# Parse 40-char hex fingerprints, normalise to upper case, drop comments.
   168	ALLOWED_FPRS=()
   169	while IFS= read -r raw_line || [[ -n "$raw_line" ]]; do
   170	    raw_line="${raw_line//$'\r'/}"
   171	    raw_line="${raw_line%%#*}"
   172	    fpr="${raw_line//[[:space:]]/}"
   173	    [[ -z "$fpr" ]] && continue
   174	    upper="$(printf '%s' "$fpr" | tr '[:lower:]' '[:upper:]')"
   175	    if [[ "$upper" =~ ^[0-9A-F]{40}$ ]]; then
   176	        ALLOWED_FPRS+=("$upper")
   177	    else
   178	        err "signers syntax error in $SIGNERS: '$raw_line' is not a valid 40-char hex GPG fingerprint"
   179	    fi
   180	done < "$SIGNERS"
   181	
   182	if [[ "${#ALLOWED_FPRS[@]}" -eq 0 ]]; then
   183	    err "$SIGNERS contains zero trusted fingerprints. Add a real release-signer fingerprint via PR, or deploy a cosign-verified image digest instead."
   184	fi
   185	
   186	# Final tags are annotated + signed; verify the tag object's signature.
   187	target="$REF"
   188	verify_cmd=(git verify-tag --raw "$target")
   189	
   190	# `git verify-*` writes GPG status to stderr; --raw emits machine-
   191	# readable "[GNUPG:] ..." lines. Capture both streams.
   192	set +e
   193	verify_out="$("${verify_cmd[@]}" 2>&1)"
   194	verify_rc=$?
   195	set -e
   196	
   197	# Find the first VALIDSIG line and extract the long fingerprint
   198	# (field 3 of "[GNUPG:] VALIDSIG <fpr> <date> ..."). grep exits 1 when
   199	# no match — under `set -o pipefail` that would silently kill the
   200	# whole script via `set -e`, so disable pipefail for this assignment
   201	# and let the empty-string branch below produce the user-facing error.
   202	set +o pipefail
   203	signer_fpr="$(
   204	    printf '%s\n' "$verify_out" \
   205	        | grep -E '^\[GNUPG:\] VALIDSIG ' \
   206	        | head -n1 \
   207	        | awk '{print toupper($3)}'
   208	)"
   209	set -o pipefail
   210	
   211	if [[ -z "$signer_fpr" ]]; then
   212	    printf '%s\n' "$verify_out" >&2
   213	    err "tag '$target' is not GPG-signed (or the signature could not be verified — git verify-tag exit=$verify_rc, no [GNUPG:] VALIDSIG line). Sign the final tag with a key listed in $SIGNERS."
   214	fi
   215	
   216	for fpr in "${ALLOWED_FPRS[@]}"; do
   217	    if [[ "$fpr" == "$signer_fpr" ]]; then
   218	        ok "Layer 2: tag '$target' signed by trusted fingerprint $signer_fpr"
   219	        exit 0
   220	    fi

deletions / 0 additions，no collateral。(e) **Step 3 commit**：`5925431 fix(ci-A): remove pnpm/action-setup version: 9 (dual-spec w/ packageManager)`，commit message 含 CI error log 引用 + 受影響 6 個 job 清單 + 設計決策（為何保 `packageManager` 而非 `with.version`）+ 3-trailer。**未動到的部分** — `package.json` 的 `packageManager` 欄位與其他屬性 byte-identical 保留；`.nvmrc` 不動；`pnpm-lock.yaml` 不動；其他 10 個 workflow 檔（`blue-green-gate` / pnpm` 保留，無需改）；沒加 pre-commit hook 擋未來類似 dual-spec（屬 L3 future）。**設計考量** — (1) **為何保 `packageManager` 而非 `with.version`**：`packageManager` 是 Node.js / Corepack 官方推薦的 package-manager 宣告方式，被 `npm` `yarn` `pnpm` 所有主流 CLI 尊重（不只 GitHub Actions），IDE、dependabot、renovate 也會讀；`with.version` 只被這個特定 GitHub Action 讀，語意較窄。(2) **為何寫 9.15.4 精確版而非 `9` 主版**：`packageManager` 支援 semver 精確；改嚴比改鬆安全——未來若 pnpm 9 的 minor 版本出 breaking change（歷史上 pnpm 8.x→9.x 確實有 breaking），精確版本能讓 CI 紅在特定 commit 而非隨機浮動。(3) **為何一次修 9 處而非拆多個 commit**：9 處都是同一根因（dual-spec）、同一 fix（移除 `with.version`），拆會讓 reviewer 跑 9 次 pnpm/action-setup 檢查；一次 commit 反映「single root cause + uniform mechanical fix」更清晰。(4) **為何 `fallback-branches.yml` 的 Edit 需帶 `- name: setup node` prefix**：該處 YAML 結構與其他 8 處不同（有額外 `name:` label），用最小區塊 diff 保證只動目標行、不誤刪 label。(5) **為何不同時移除下游 `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --prefer-offline`**：這些步驟與 version conflict 無關，動它們會擴 scope 變成 perf 優化 PR。(6) **為何不擴到 `setup-node` 的 `cache: pnpm` 欄位**：該欄位告訴 `actions/setup-node` 去找 pnpm 的 store path 做 cache，與 `pnpm/action-setup@v4` 的版本衝突無關。(7) **為何審計 A 類同時發現而本 row 只修 A**：`docs/sop/fix_bug_step.md` 明確「嚴格按照順序執行，不可以跳步驟」，SOP Step 2「拆分子階段」針對單一 root cause，跨 root cause 合併違反 scope discipline；B 類涉及 stdlib shadow（機構性變更）、C 類涉及 renovate schema 修正（不同領域），合併 commit message 會變 Frankenstein。(8) **為何 CI annotation 說「CI config 'version' key」而本 repo 寫 `with.version`**：pnpm/action-setup@v4 的 input 叫 `version`，該 action 的 runtime 把它歸類為 "GitHub Action config"——這是 action 的命名習慣，與 repo 的 `with:` yaml key 在語義上是同一物。**驗收 / 自評** — (i) `git log --oneline -2` 顯示 `5925431 fix(ci-A)` + `c9b6860 docs(HANDOFF): log Fix api-error-handler`；(ii) YAML syntax 5/5 clean；(iii) `version: 9` 0 出現；(iv) 9 pnpm setup + 9 pnpm install 皆保留；(v) commit 僅 5 檔 / 9 -1 lines / 無 collateral / 3-trailer；(vi) SOP start」轉為「能實際跑」——剩下的錯誤若有才是真 issue。**下一手要做的** — 按 CI log 三類分工：(A) **Fix B**（backend-tests 4 shards exit 2 — `backend/platform.py` shadow stdlib）：改 `ci.yml` backend-tests job 從 repo root 跑 pytest + SIGTERM`、`stop_grace_period: 60s`）。drain 順序必須「ingress first → frontend → backend → workers」——先擋外部新流量再等 in-flight 清掉，workers 晚於 backend 是因為 backend 剛把任務丟進 queue 時若先殺 worker 會孤兒化。健康端點 `/readyz` + installed、否則 compose）、`--compose-file <path>`（prod 預設，無則 dev）、`--timeout <sec>`（≥40，預設 90）、`--backup-db`（stop backend **前** `sqlite3 .backup` 確保 WAL 熱備一致）、`--skip-ingress`（rolling 用）、`--dry-run`（前綴 `+` shell-x 風格列印、不執行）、`--force`（服務已停/缺失不 fail）；systemd 流程：cloudflared → frontend → backup → backend → 所有 `omnisight-worker@N`（用 `systemctl list-units --all --type=service` 枚舉）→ `verify_systemd_down` 全數確認 inactive；compose 流程：caddy → frontend → backup → backends 列（backend / backend-a / backend-b 都會偵測到的才停）→ worker（`--profile workers`）→ 觀測 sidecar；exit codes 0=done / 1=未在 grace 內停 / 2=缺 prerequisite / 3=參數錯；所有 `sudo systemctl` + docker 指令都走 `run` wrapper 接 dry-run；`compose_cmd()` 自動解析 v2 `docker compose` vs 老 `docker-compose`。(b) 新增 `scripts/restart.sh` 260 行：`--mode {systemd|compose|rolling|blue-green|auto}`、`--skip-backup`（預設會備份、可關）、`--env {staging|prod}`（forward 給 deploy.sh）、`--timeout`、`--dry-run`；systemd 流程：備份 → **delegate 到 `scripts/shutdown.sh --mode systemd`**（避免邏輯重複）→ 依序 start backend + poll `http://127.0.0.1:8000/readyz` until 2xx（`curl -sSf` + 2 s retry）→ start 每個 `enabled` 的 `omnisight-worker@N`（running-but-not-enabled 視為 operator 實驗品不動）→ start frontend + poll → start cloudflared（tunnel **最後**開，避免外部流量打到還沒 ready 的 backend）；compose 流程：備份 → shutdown → `docker compose up -d` → poll 雙副本的 /readyz 或單副本的 /readyz；rolling / blue-green 一律 `exec` 到 `scripts/deploy.sh --strategy {rolling,blue-green} <env>`（G2/G3 已有 test 覆蓋、不重造）；exit codes 加 4=readiness poll timeout（讓 CI 可分辨「啟動失敗」vs「啟動成功但健檢未綠」）。(c) 新增 `docs/operations/shutdown-restart.md` 7 區塊 runbook：**§1 Inventory** 三套拓撲的服務 × grace period × drain 行為表；**§2 shutdown.sh** 用法 × 四條 ordering invariants × exit codes × underlying 機制（SIGTERM → TimeoutStopSec → SIGKILL + lifecycle.py / worker.py 行碼引用）；**§3 restart.sh** 模式矩陣（systemd/compose/rolling/blue-green）× 起動順序 × 健檢輪詢 × exit codes；**§4 Recovery** 四種異常場景 — backend 卡 drain（`_startup_cleanup` 在 `backend/main.py:13-41` 下次啟動自動 reset）/ worker 卡 60 s（visibility timer + 30min lock TTL 自動復原）/ 健檢不轉綠（先看 `validate_startup_config()` raise / Redis-backed `/readyz`）/ rolling 中斷（Caddy 留活副本、修完再跑）/ blue-green 失敗（跑 `deploy.sh --rollback`）；**§5 Troubleshooting** 五行症狀對照；**§6 Validation checklist** 操作員上線前勾選；**§7 Related** 連回 deployment.md / blue_green_runbook.md / deploy.sh / lifecycle.py / worker.py 行碼。**驗證** — (i) `bash -n scripts/shutdown.sh` + `bash -n scripts/restart.sh` 雙雙 syntax OK；(ii) `shellcheck` 此環境未安裝，跳過（已列 validation checklist 第 3 項給 operator 自檢）；(iii) `scripts/shutdown.sh --dry-run` auto-detect 走 compose 模式（本機無 systemctl、有 docker）、輸出 `[shutdown] mode: compose (resolved from 'auto')`、整條 stack 已非運行 → 直接 `compose stack is down`；(iv) `scripts/shutdown.sh --mode compose --compose-file docker-compose.yml --backup-db --dry-run` 正確 fallthrough `sqlite3 not installed` warning 並續走；(v) `scripts/shutdown.sh --timeout 20` 正確 exit 3 + 訊息 `--timeout must be an integer ≥ 40 (backend drain needs 40s minimum)`；(vi) `scripts/restart.sh --mode compose --compose-file docker-compose.yml --dry-run` 正確呼叫 shutdown.sh、再印出 `+ docker compose -f docker-compose.yml up -d` + 兩次 poll `+ poll http://127.0.0.1:8000/readyz`（dry-run 下 poll 跳過）。**設計考量** — (1) **為何 restart.sh delegate shutdown.sh 而非內嵌邏輯**：避免雙實作漂移——stop 語意只在一個地方（ordering / grace / verify），restart 只是「stop + start + poll」的 orchestrator；(2) **為何預設備份 DB 在 restart 但 shutdown 預設不備份**：restart 幾乎一定是部署/修改觸發（需要 roll-back fallback），停機則可能是單純維護窗口（operator 可能才剛備份過）——`--backup-db` / `--skip-backup` 語意對稱但預設值對齊最常見意圖；(3) **為何 `--timeout` 最小值卡 40**：低於這數字 backend drain 會被硬殺 → 丟 in-flight request；給 operator 一個 guardrail 比留地雷合理；(4) **為何 rolling/blue-green 用 `exec` 而非 subprocess**：deploy.sh 的 exit code + stdout/stderr 是 CI 對齊的契約，exec 讓 restart.sh 完全隱形（no wrapper）；(5) **為何 worker 用 `enabled` filter 而非 `active`**：stop 需要遍歷「曾經啟用但現在可能停了」的全部；start 只應叫醒「設計上希望它起來」的那些（enabled）——running-but-not-enabled 是人為實驗，restart.sh 不該主張；(6) **為何 ingress 最後 start**：防止 tunnel 在 backend 還在跑 migration / startup_cleanup 的 5-10 秒窗口把 404 / 502 推給外部客戶——用戶寧可多等 5 秒看 maintenance page 也不要看 5xx；(7) **為何不自動跑 `systemd-analyze verify` on the unit files**：這腳本只管 runtime，不管 unit 檔正確性（那是部署階段 deploy.sh / ansible 的責任）；(8) **為何 compose shutdown 不用 `down` 而用一連串 `stop`**：`down` 會一併砍 network，下次 `up -d` 要重建 network 慢約 1-2 秒；純 `stop` 保留 network / volume / image，restart 更快；且保留便於 `docker compose logs` 事後查詢；(9) **為何把 `/readyz` poll 延遲上限給 90 s 而非 30 s**：startup cleanup（`backend/main.py:13-41` 重置卡住 agents / simulations、清 orphan containers、清 git lock、seed defaults、restore decision rules/profiles、bootstrap admin、migrate legacy bearer）在 crash-recovery 場景可能跑 20-40 秒，加 DB migration 可能破 60；90 s 給足餘裕；(10) **為何 exit 4 單獨編號**：CI 想區分「沒啟動」（code 1，要看 logs）vs「啟動了但 health probe 沒綠」（code 4，可能只是網路慢 / 可以再 poll 而非立即 rollback）。**沒做什麼**（刻意，scope 紀律）— (1) 沒動 `auto-runner.py` 一個字（user 明確劃出系統邊界外）；(2) 沒改既有 `scripts/deploy.sh` / `scripts/clean-prod-env.sh` / `scripts/quick-start.sh`（restart rolling / blue-green mode delegate 進去即可，deploy.sh 內部邏輯是 G2/G3 測試契約不動）；(3) 沒改任何 systemd unit 檔（`deploy/systemd/*.service` 的 `TimeoutStopSec` 已經正確，shutdown.sh 只是調度者）；(4) 沒改 `docker-compose*.yml`（worker 的 `stop_grace_period: 60s` 已設、backends 有 healthcheck 已足）；(5) 沒新增 pytest / bash-test（shellcheck 建議放 CI validation checklist 給 operator；短期靠 `bash -n` + `--dry-run` 驗）；(6) 沒加 systemd `sudoers.d/omnisight` passwordless 規則（安全議題分開審查，docs §5 有列建議）；(7) 沒改 `/readyz` / `/healthz` 實作；(8) 沒 commit—user 還沒要求；(9) 沒動 TODO.md（不是 TODO row，是 ops tooling）；(10) 沒動 backend/ 任何檔案。**下一步建議** — (A) **加進 CI**：`.github/workflows/shell-lint.yml` 跑 `shellcheck scripts/*.sh` 覆蓋新增兩個檔，避免未來 regression；(B) **sudoers.d/omnisight**：限定 operator 對 `systemctl {start,stop,restart} omnisight-*` passwordless，避免 restart.sh 在有人值守的維護窗口卡 sudo prompt；(C) **pytest for bash**：用 bats-core 或 pytest + `subprocess`-based 測試 shutdown/restart 的 `--dry-run` 輸出，凍結 ordering invariants；(D) **`scripts/status.sh` 姊妹腳本**：一鍵列 `systemctl status omnisight-*` + `docker compose ps` + `/healthz` + `/readyz` + `/livez` 的合成儀表板，operator 判斷 shutdown/restart 是否需要時用；(E) **Prometheus alert server 端拒絕；三 job 並行平均跑時間 = max(5, 5) + 15 = 20 min worst case，合一 sequential 是 25 min（節省 5 min/PR），但合一就失去 fail-fast diagnostic value；(b) **`--dry-run=server` 不 `--dry-run=client`**：client 端只走 OpenAPI schema 不走 admission webhook，捕不到「v1.29 admission policy 拒絕某 field」這類深層問題；server 走 K8s API 真實 admission chain，是「render + apply cleanly」承諾的唯一誠實實現；(c) **不真的 apply**：placeholder image `your-org/omnisight-backend:latest` pull 不到，真的 apply 會 ImagePullBackOff 卡 wait timeout；server-dry-run 既驗證 manifest 又不需真 image — 真實 deploy 的 readiness 是 G3 blue-green smoke 的事，G5 #6 是 manifest 層的 contract；(d) **Gateway-API toggle 用第四次 render 而非第二個 cluster**：toggle 是 chart template 層 `if/else` 互斥 `Ingress` vs `HTTPRoute`，render 階段就決定的事不需要真 cluster；第四次 render + `grep` HTTPRoute 字面是 charter §7.6 explicit toggle 的最便宜 contract；(e) **kind cluster 名固定 `omnisight-g5-smoke`**：固定名讓 `kind export logs` 命令在 failure step 可預測；workflow concurrency.group 已 per-ref cancel-in-progress 不會兩 run 撞同名；(f) **contract test 跨界檢查 G6 dr_drill + G7 grafana**：G5 #6 是 G5 closure，下一 bucket 是 G6 / G7；本 commit landed CI workflow 後若有人「順手」也加 G6 dr_drill 或 G7 grafana 字面，即 silent 偷跑下一 bucket scope — guard 強制下次 G6/G7 commit 必須同步 flip 對應 row + 刪 assert（顯性遷移 pattern 從 G5 #3→#4→#5→#6 內部延續至 G5→G6/G7 bucket 邊界）；(g) **README 不寫「CI 跑了所以 local diff 不必要」**：local diff 是 operator 在 push 前 self-check 的工具、CI 是 author push 後的 enforcement —— 兩者互補不替代；保留 local 命令給 staging operator 在 internal cluster 比 prod 即將 apply 的 manifest；(h) **三 README 都改 + 加 NOT-included G6/G7 bullet**：G5 是 G5 bucket closure（最後一 row），future reader 看 README 應該一眼看到「G5 全 closed, G6/G7 是下一 bucket」 — 不放 G6/G7 bullet，5 年後 reader silent 重 design 已 decided 的 scope；(i) **跨 helm-render+kind-smoke 鎖 staging/prod overlay 出現次數 ≥2**：render 端綠不代表 server 端綠（client OpenAPI schema 落後 K8s admission policy 2-3 個 minor），必須兩端各跑一次才完整覆蓋；contract `text.count(...) >= 2` 是「render-only sin」的反 regression 鎖；(j) **HELM_VERSION + KUBECTL_VERSION + KIND_NODE_IMAGE 三 application_name=omnisight_standby'` + `primary_slot_name='omnisight_standby_slot'` + `chmod 0600` 讓密碼輪替只需 restart；`exec postgres -c config_file=/etc/postgresql/postgresql.conf -c only）。**為什麼 sync 是 operator opt-in 不是預設** — sync 加 2-5 ms p50 latency（本地網路）或 20-50 ms p50（跨 AZ）對 OmniSight 大部分 audit_log / decision_log write path 不划算；留給 operator 按 compliance window 自行打開。G4 #3 checkbox 規範「synchronous_commit=on 可設」的意義不是硬開，而是**讓 operator 能一行 config edit + pg_ctl reload 開關**，這正是我們做的 — primary conf 顯式宣告 `synchronous_commit = on`（primary-side durability）+ `synchronous_standby_names = ''`（replication mode 切換鍵），配合 .env.example 的 operator guide block 三處 documented entry point。**為什麼 pg_hba 用 scram-sha-256 全線** — md5 從 PG 14 deprecated，wire 上 leak password hash；scram-sha-256 是 PG 14+ default + `primary_conninfo` 認證等同 channel。**為什麼用 named physical slot 不只靠 wal_keep_size** — slot 保證 primary 等 standby consumed WAL 才釋放即便 segment 到了 1 GB；wal_keep_size 只是 best-effort retention window，長斷線會觸發 re-basebackup。**為什麼 init-standby 每次 boot 重寫 postgresql.auto.conf** — 密碼輪替 operator 更新 `.env` + `docker compose restart pg-standby` 就生效，不需 `docker exec` 進 container；`chmod 0600` 確保 host 上即使被誤 cat 至少 owner-only。**為什麼 standby override container entrypoint 不走 initdb.d** — docker-entrypoint.sh 會先 `initdb` 空 PGDATA 然後跑 initdb.d；我們要**反過來**先 pg_basebackup 再 signal，所以只能覆寫 entrypoint 用自己的 init-standby.sh 接手。**為什麼刻意不把 primary_conninfo 塞進 postgresql.standby.conf** — 該檔 commit 進 git，塞密碼 = leak；runtime 寫入 postgresql.auto.conf 才是 PG 16 正確模式。**契約測試 114 條 0.33 s config_file flag + passwords fail-closed）、(6) init-primary 10、(7) init-standby 15、(8) env example 14、(9) parse_pg_conf 8、(10) parse_hba 3、(11) verifier end-to-end 5（真跑 `python3 scripts/pg_ha_verify.py` 兩次 human+json + **negative gates 3**：砍 primary wal_level → exit 1、加 md5 row → exit 1、加 remote trust row → exit 1）、(12) symmetry 5（slot name 'omnisight_standby_slot' 四處 / application_name 'omnisight_standby' 四處含 primary conf comment / replicator user 三處 / postgres:16-alpine / sync-commit 三處 documentation chain — 解決「primary 寫 slot X 但 standby 用 slot Y」silent mis-routing）。**活體驗證** — (a) `python3 scripts/pg_ha_verify.py` 75/75 checks OK；(b) `python3 -m pytest backend/tests/test_pg_ha_deployment.py -q` → 114 passed 0.33 s；(c) 全量 G4 回歸 `test_pg_ha_deployment `backend-a` / `backend-b` 兩副本（共用 volume）」* — 完成。**做了什麼** — 把 `docker-compose.prod.yml` 原本單一 `backend` service 拆成 `backend-a`（container port 8000）+ `backend-b`（container port 8001）兩個對稱副本，兩者共用 `omnisight-data` / `omnisight-artifacts` / `omnisight-sdks` 三個 named volume（SQLite DB + artefacts + cached SDKs）讓請求可落在任一副本，mount 同樣四個（再加 `/var/run/docker.sock`）且同樣 env_file: .env、restart: always、OMNISIGHT_ENV=production / AUTH_MODE=strict / DEBUG=false。port 契約對齊 G2 #1 Caddyfile 的 upstream 字面量 `backend-a:8000, backend-b:8001`：backend-a 吃 Dockerfile baked-in CMD（`--port 8000`），backend-b 用 compose-level `command:` override 直接改成 `--port 8001`、同步 healthcheck 改打 `http://localhost:8001/readyz` 且兩者都保留 `start_period: 20s` 讓 first-boot migration 有緩衝；兩副本分別注入 `OMNISIGHT_INSTANCE_ID=backend-a|backend-b` 讓 log/metric 能 trace 回具體副本。frontend 改 `depends_on: {backend-a: service_healthy, backend-b: service_healthy}`、`BACKEND_URL=http://backend-a:8000`（build arg + env 同步）以 backend-a 為預設 SSR 上游；prometheus sidecar 的 `depends_on` 從 `backend` 擴成 `backend-a + backend-b`。host port 8000:8000 + 8001:8001 分別對外 expose，讓 operator 可在 rolling-restart 中以 `curl localhost:8000/readyz` / `curl localhost:8001/readyz` 直接 smoke 兩副本、不需透過 Caddy。**一個關鍵的 escape-semantic 陷阱** — backend-b 的 `command:` 需要在 container 內 shell 跑 `WORKERS=${OMNISIGHT_WORKERS:-$(python3 -c 'import os; print(max(2, os.cpu_count() // 2))')}; exec python -m uvicorn ... --workers $WORKERS`（和 Dockerfile.backend CMD 同公式）；但 docker-compose 會在 config-resolve 階段先做 `${...}` 插值、把 `OMNISIGHT_WORKERS` 當成 host env 取值（未設時套用 default），等於把整段 shell fallback 邏輯吃掉、container 內拿到的是硬編碼值、喪失 OMNISIGHT_WORKERS override 能力。第一版寫單一 `$`、驗 `docker compose config` 發現被吃；第二版改成 **每個需要留到 container shell 執行的 `$` 全數 double 成 `$$`**（`$${OMNISIGHT_WORKERS:-$$(python3 -c '...')}` / `$$WORKERS`），再驗 rendered YAML 確認 `$$` 存留、代表 compose 傳給 container 的是字面 `${...}` / `$(...)` / `$WORKER`，由 container `sh -c` 真正解析 — 這是 compose 文件內對 shell-exec-level interpolation 必踩的 escape 約定。**為什麼拆成兩 service 而非用 `deploy.replicas: 2`** — compose 的 `deploy.replicas` 是 Swarm-only 語意、`docker compose up` 不讀；即便讀了，兩副本會共用同一 service 名稱 + port，無法對 Caddyfile upstream stanza 的 `backend-a:8000 backend-b:8001` 字面名稱契約、也無法對 host 分別 expose 8000 / 8001。兩獨立 service + distinct ports 是 compose-native 最直接的作法。**legacy DNS alias** — `backend-a.networks.default.aliases: [backend]` 讓舊 `configs/prometheus.yml` 的 scrape target `backend:8000` 零改動仍可達（這次 scope 鐵律：**只改 docker-compose.prod.yml**，prometheus.yml 留給下 commit 擴成 scrape 兩 replicas 再移除 alias）。**共用 volume 的 SQLite 注意事項（closed-loop note）** — G2 這層仍是 SQLite、兩 writer 競爭會觸發 `database is locked`，G4（HA-04）會遷到 PostgreSQL 真正解放併發；G2 的 rolling-restart flow 在 cutover 窗內只有一副本活著、實務上能規避，acceptable for plumbing-level HA。**契約測試 31 條新增（0.10 s）** — `backend/tests/test_compose_dual_backend_replicas.py`（~320 行）分 7 個測試類：(1) `TestReplicasDeclared` 3 條 — backend-a/backend-b service string templating**（`fromService` 只能取 sibling 的 host / port / hostport，**不能**像 DO 的 `${APP_URL}` 或 Railway 的 `${{frontend.RAILWAY_PUBLIC_DOMAIN}}` 這樣組 `http://...` 或 `https://...` 完整 URL）。**三個 CSRF/CORS middleware bypass 掉形成安全退化（契約測試 `test_backend_is_private_pserv` pin 死）；(2) **pserv 不會注入 `$PORT`**（與 Railway web service 不同），所以 `dockerCommand` 保留 `--port 8000` 而非 `${PORT:-8000}`，frontend 的 web service 才由 Render 注入 `$PORT`（Next.js standalone 的 `node server.js` 自動讀 `process.env.PORT`），兩 service 行為不對稱需在 runbook 明示；(3) **env var 跨 service templating 限制** → 解法三叉：(a) `BACKEND_URL=http://omnisight-backend:8000` 硬 pin 在 frontend envVars（pserv 內部 hostname == service name 的 Render 服務發現契約，契約測試 `test_frontend_hard_pins_backend_url` 驗精確字串），(b) `OMNISIGHT_FRONTEND_ORIGIN` + `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` 改用 `sync: false` 讓 Render Blueprint wizard 在 apply 時提示 operator 手填，(c) **Stage 2 redeploy 流程**：Stage 1 第一次 deploy 只填 secrets 把 app 起起來、Stage 2 從 dashboard 抄 `*.onrender.com` URL → 設兩個 URL env → manual redeploy（強調 frontend 必須 rebuild 因為 `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` 是 build-time inlined 到 client bundle）。**secret hygiene 模式切換** — DO 用 `type: SECRET` + sentinel gerrit-sora/develop:HANDOFF.md:28866:> **前一回合（L10 #5 — 「pull 30 秒」effect capstone 契約測試，收尾 L10）**：L10「Pre-built Docker Images on GHCR (#337)」最末兩顆 checkbox（「效果：首次部署從本地 build 5-10 分鐘 → pull 30 秒」+「預估 0.5 day」）。**背景** — L10 #1-#4 已經交付四個 precondition：#1 workflow 發佈 `ghcr.io/<owner>/omnisight-{backend,frontend}:latest + v*` 到 GHCR、#2 `docker-compose.prod.yml` image-first（`image:` + `pull_policy: missing` + `build:` fallback）、#3 `docker buildx` multi-arch 同時推 amd64 + arm64 OCI image index、#4 image size 優化到 backend 490 MB / frontend 169 MB uncompressed（壓縮傳輸 bytes 為 103 + 68 = 171 MiB）。這個 capstone 項目 pin 住**四個 precondition 同時成立所衍生的 emergent effect**——「首次部署 = compose pull（~27 秒）而非 local build（5-10 分鐘）」。**做了什麼** — 新增單一檔案 `backend/tests/test_pull_30s_effect.py`（~260 行、5 靜態 + 1 live opt-in）延續 L10 sibling 測試的 `yaml.safe_load + 結構性 assertion` 模式，無網路無 Docker daemon、< 100 ms 跑完。**關鍵單位敏感性**——效果 = 壓縮 bytes ÷ bandwidth，而 `50 Mbps = 50 × 10⁶ bits/s`（network decimal）≠ `50 × 2²⁰ / 8 bytes/s`（storage binary）相差約 **4.86%**；測試顯式把 `_BITS_PER_MB_NET = 1_000_000`（decimal）與 `_BYTES_PER_MIB = 1024 × 1024`（binary）兩個常數寫清楚，`BANDWIDTH_BYTES_PER_SEC = 50 × 10⁶ / 8 ≈ 6_250_000 bytes/s`，`PULL_BUDGET_BYTES = 6_250_000 × 30 = 187_500_000 ≈ 178.8 MiB` envelope。envelope 看似充裕、實測 171 MiB combined 只剩 **7.8 MiB slack**（≈ 4.4% margin）——這比我一開始直覺寫的「120 + 75 = 195 MiB budget」緊很多，**測試當場抓到這個 budget 算術不自洽**（195 > 178.8）並丟出含「shrink image OR update 30 秒 promise in TODO.md+HANDOFF.md」的錯誤訊息，修正到 `108 + 70 = 178 MiB` 才過——**這正是契約測試該做的事：把 implicit promise 逼成 explicit arithmetic，讓偏差一目了然**。**五個靜態測試**：(1) `test_pull_budget_arithmetic_is_internally_consistent` — pin 50 Mbps × 30 s → ~178.8 MiB envelope 的 conversion chain；sanity 斷言 envelope 落在 150-200 MiB 帶，decimal point 被搬移此處先炸。(2) `test_combined_image_budgets_fit_the_30s_envelope` — **load-bearing**：`BACKEND_COMPRESSED_BUDGET_MIB=108` + `FRONTEND_COMPRESSED_BUDGET_MIB=70` 合計 178 MiB ≤ 178.8 MiB envelope；budget 緊貼實測基線 103 + 68 MiB 的「+5 / +2」小 headroom（避免正常 dependency bump 噪音）但一旦 dev venv 洩漏或 Alpine base 被撤換就炸。(3) `test_pull_path_reachable_via_compose_image_first` — parse `docker-compose.prod.yml`、backend+frontend 都 assert `image:` 以 `ghcr.io/` 起頭 + `pull_policy: missing`（擋 drift 到 `always`／每次強制 re-pull 或 `build`／完全繞開 pull path）。(4) `test_quickstart_does_not_force_build_on_compose_up` — 逐行掃 `scripts/quick-start.sh`、過濾 `#` 註解與 `echo` user-facing copy（line 1061「強制本地 build」逃生口 echo 合法提到 `--build`），assert 沒有真實的 `docker compose ... up --build` invocation——`--build` 是**最容易靜默 regression 30 秒承諾的單一 flag**，與 L10 #2 既有測試 defence-in-depth 並存。(5) `test_workflow_actually_pushes_so_pull_is_possible` — parse `docker-publish.yml`、逐 job/step 找 `docker/build-push-action` 並 assert `with.push: true`（接受 boolean True 或 str "true"），擋「為了 test workflow 暫時關掉 publish」這類 CI-green-prod-broken regression。**Live opt-in**：`test_live_combined_pull_under_30s_at_50mbps` — 與 L10 #4 共享 env gate（`OMNISIGHT_TEST_DOCKER_IMAGE_SIZE=1`）+ 同 helper（`docker image inspect --format '{{.Size}}'`），取 backend + frontend 實際 bytes ÷ `BANDWIDTH_BYTES_PER_SEC` 得 wall-clock 秒數 assert ≤ 30 s；image 不存在時 `pytest.skip` 乾淨跳過不炸 CI lane。**為什麼是 5 個測試而不是 1 個**——四個 precondition 中任何一個靜默 regression 都會讓「pull 30 秒」承諾失效（pull_policy 改 always 會每次重拉；push: false 會讓 registry 空、compose pull 404 fallback build；image 膨脹 20% 會超 envelope；quick-start 加回 `--build` 直接繞開 pull）——**把 failure mode 拆開分別 assert** 讓 CI 錯誤訊息直接指向 regression 的 root cause 而不是「效果消失了」。**結果** `python3 -m pytest backend/tests/test_pull_30s_effect.py backend/tests/test_dockerfile_image_size.py backend/tests/test_compose_prod_image_first.py backend/tests/test_docker_publish_workflow.py -v` → **49 passed + 3 skipped in 0.21s**（5 新 static + 44 prior static + 3 live opt-in 共通路徑，零 regression）；`tests/test_quick_start_script.py` **51 passed in 7.04s**（完全不受影響，沒動 quick-start.sh）。**Scope 自律** — 只新增一個 test 檔；沒動 Dockerfile / compose / workflow / quick-start / .env.example / 任何應用碼——「效果」本身已由 L10 #1-#4 交付，此項目把**四個 precondition 串成的 emergent contract 變成 automated**，讓任何靜默 regression 都會炸 CI。**L10 整區完成** — 6 顆 checkbox 全綠（workflow + compose image-first + multi-arch + image size + 效果 capstone + 預估）；共累積 61 個契約測試（publish workflow 16 + compose image-first 13 + Dockerfile size 17 + pull 30 秒 effect 6 + 既有 sibling 9），任何一個 precondition 改動都會有對應測試發聲。**Operator 下一步** — 打 `v*` tag 觸發 docker-publish workflow（QEMU 雙架構 build、~10-15 min）→ GHCR 出現 combined ~170 MiB 壓縮 bytes → 真實部署主機 `export OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE=<lowercase-owner>` 後 `docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml up -d` 觸發 compose image-first path 從 ghcr.io 拉取，50 Mbps 下行預期 **~27 s** 完成（對照 L10 前「本地 Python wheels + Next.js 15 build 5-10 分鐘」→ 改善 **~15×**）。**後續**：L11（Deploy 按鈕 #338 — DigitalOcean / Railway / Render 三家 one-click deploy YAML + README badge）為下一階段。
gerrit-sora/develop:HANDOFF.md:28870:> **前一回合（L10 #4 — Image size 優化：backend 1.34 GB → 490 MB / frontend 272 MB → 169 MB + 17 顆契約測試）**：L10「Pre-built Docker Images on GHCR (#337)」第 4 顆 checkbox。**做了什麼** — 針對 backend < 500 MB / frontend < 200 MB 的預算，把兩個 image 從原始的 1.34 GB / 272 MB 壓到 **490 MB / 169 MB**（unpacked filesystem，`du -sb` 量），對應到 registry 真正交付的 **compressed pull size 103 MB / 68 MB**（`docker image inspect --format '{{.Size}}'`），剛好吃進「pull 30 秒」承諾的容量（50 Mbps × 30 s ≈ 180 MB，combined 171 MB 吞得下）。**四大優化並用**：(1) **`.dockerignore` 遞迴 patterns** — 單一最大 bug：原檔用 bare `.venv` / `__pycache__` / `node_modules`，Docker 的 dockerignore 語意是 `.venv` 只匹配 `./.venv`，**不含** `./backend/.venv`（這個 dev venv 168 MB 的 duplicate site-packages 一直靜默洩漏進 backend image）；修正是把所有 cache/venv 類 pattern 改成 `**/pattern` 形式，新增 `**/.venv`、`**/__pycache__`、`**/node_modules`、`**/.pytest_cache`、`**/.mypy_cache`、`**/.pnpm-store` 等；`.dockerignore` 開頭註解寫清楚為什麼要 `**/` prefix 以免未來「清理」回 bare pattern 再炸。(2) **Dockerfile.backend 多階段化** — 原本單階段把 gcc/g++/git 塞進 runtime，改成 `builder` stage 跑 `pip install --require-hashes -r requirements.txt`（需要 gcc 編 uvloop/pillow/cryptography/argon2-cffi/hiredis 等 native wheel），`runner` stage 只 `apt-get install` weasyprint 的執行期 libs（libpango-1.0-0 / libpangoft2-1.0-0 / libcairo2 / libgdk-pixbuf-2.0-0 / libffi8 / shared-mime-info / fonts-dejavu-core + curl），用 `COPY --from=builder /usr/local/lib/python3.12/site-packages /usr/local/lib/python3.12/site-packages` 把已裝好的 Python packages 傳過去。**關鍵陷阱**：最初嘗試 `pip wheel` + `pip install --no-index --find-links=/wheels` 失敗——`pip wheel` 把 sdist 編成 wheel 時，wheel 的 hash 與 lockfile 記錄的 pypi wheel/sdist hash 不同，`--require-hashes` 直接拒收（`zxcvbn-python` 等純 sdist 套件炸）；改用「builder 直接 install 到 system site-packages，runner COPY 整份過來」，hash 契約完整。`test_backend_does_not_use_pip_wheel_pattern` 把這個決定 pin 住。(3) **Dropping test + bytecode + WOFF2 deadcode** — builder 內三波 cleanup：`pip uninstall -y pytest pytest-asyncio pytest-cov coverage iniconfig pluggy`（~25 MB；prod code 已驗證不 import pytest）、`rm -rf site-packages/{pip,pip-*.dist-info,zstandard*,zopfli*}`（zstandard 23 MB + zopfli 2.7 MB 是 fontTools[woff] 的 WOFF2 壓縮 extra，weasyprint 跑 PDF pipeline 不走 WOFF2，已用 `weasyprint.HTML(...).write_pdf()` 確認 PDF 輸出 bytes-identical）、bytecode cache 全清。runner 又追一份 `rm -rf` 打掉 python:3.12-slim base 自帶的 pip + `/usr/share/{doc,man,bash-completion}` + `rm -rf ./backend/tests ./backend/.pytest_cache`（31 MB）。(4) **Dockerfile.frontend 換 base** — 原本 runner 用 `node:20-alpine`（含 npm/yarn/corepack ~22 MB，但 `node server.js` 從不呼叫 package manager）；發現「`RUN rm -rf` npm/corepack」沒用——Docker layer additive、刪檔只產 whiteout marker，image bytes 不縮；切成「builder 用 `node:20-alpine` 跑 `pnpm run build`、runner 換 `alpine:3.19` + `apk add nodejs libstdc++ ca-certificates`」才真省 bytes（alpine 的 nodejs apk 是精簡 node 20.15.x，純 runtime）；builder/runner 同 musl-base，Next.js standalone bundle 直接搬過去，`timeout 5 node server.js` 看到「✓ Ready in 0ms」。**第二刀**：runner 追 `rm -rf /app/node_modules/.pnpm/@img+sharp-libvips-*` + `sharp@*`（~33 MB）——`next.config.mjs` `images: { unoptimized: true }` 已關 sharp 路徑，但 Next standalone tracer 仍保守 bundle，拆掉省 33 MB；`|| true` guard 防未來 Next 改 bundle 位置讓 rm 失效但 build 不炸。**測試** 新增 `backend/tests/test_dockerfile_image_size.py`（17 cases；15 靜態 + 2 live opt-in）—— backend：multi-stage、runner 無 gcc/g++/git、weasyprint runtime libs 在、test packages 被 uninstall、zstandard+zopfli 被 rm、pip 被剝、`./backend/tests` 被 rm、不用 `pip wheel` 模式；frontend：multi-stage、runner base = `alpine:3.x`、`ca-certificates` 保留、`sharp-libvips` 被 rm；.dockerignore：`**/.venv`、`**/__pycache__`、`**/node_modules` 三個遞迴 pattern 都在。Live opt-in 兩顆：`OMNISIGHT_TEST_DOCKER_IMAGE_SIZE=1 pytest` 才跑，透過 `docker image inspect --format '{{.Size}}'` 拿 compressed pull size assert <500 MiB / <200 MiB（多數 CI lane 沒 build image，default skip）。**結果** `test_dockerfile_image_size.py` **15 passed + 2 skipped**；sibling 全綠：3 個 L10 test 一起跑 **44 passed + 2 skipped**；`tests/test_quick_start_script.py` **51 passed**（完全不受 Dockerfile 改動影響）。**Live runtime smoke**：`docker run ... python -c 'from backend.main import app; import weasyprint; host resolve 到 amd64 layer，**一個 tag 解一切架構**（不需要讓 compose 或 deploy 腳本 pass `--platform`）；(3) `timeout-minutes: 45 → 90` —— QEMU-emulated arm64 build 比 native amd64 慢約 2×（frontend 的 `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` + Next.js 15 build 是主要 CPU sink，~8-12 min emulated arm64 vs ~4 min native；backend Python 3.12-slim 因為只 pip install 不編譯 C extension 影響較小），45 min 會 flaky-timeout 但 build 本身健康；(4) header comment 從「multi-arch 未 scope」更新為實作說明 + QEMU rationale + wall-time 預期。**為什麼不換 `runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm`（native arm64 runner）避開 QEMU** —— GitHub 在 2025 Q1 開始供應原生 arm64 runner，**但 free tier 的 public repo 目前僅對部分 org 開放**，用 `runs-on` matrix（amd64 runner build amd64 layer + arm64 runner build arm64 layer，再用 `docker buildx imagetools create` 合併成 manifest list）會讓 workflow 對 repo owner 類型產生隱性耦合（private repo / non-whitelisted org 會 queue forever），此外多 runner 方案需要 artifact handover 或 two-phase publish，workflow 複雜度上升且首次 publish 會多一次 failure mode。QEMU 路線對所有 ubuntu-latest runner 保證可跑，速度 trade-off 可接受，若未來 GHA 的 arm64 runner 對所有 repo 開放免費 quota 可再切換。**驗證** `python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/docker-publish.yml'))"` 解析通過；printed step resolution 確認 QEMU step 出現在 buildx 之前（index 3 vs 4）、`platforms: linux/amd64,linux/arm64` 字串正確寫入 build-push-action `with` block、`timeout-minutes: 90` 生效。**測試** 新增 **4 顆契約測試** 於 index，擋「好心重排 step 順序」的 regression（這 regression silent 可怕：workflow 跑得過 build 卻只出 amd64，操作者從 arm64 host pull 會拿到 amd64 image + 在 runtime 炸 exec format error）；(c) `test_build_push_publishes_amd64_and_arm64` — 讀 build-push-action 的 `with.platforms` 字串、分別 assert `linux/amd64` 和 `linux/arm64` 都在，錯誤訊息明確寫「dropping this defeats the L10 #337 multi-arch contract」擋「為了加速 CI 暫時註記掉一個平台」的 regression；(d) `test_timeout_accommodates_emulated_arm64_build` — assert `timeout-minutes >= 60` 擋回退到 45，註解解釋 QEMU emulated frontend build ~8-12 min、需要 headroom（60 而不是 90 當底線是讓未來若真的優化了 gerrit-sora/develop:HANDOFF.md:28874:> **上一回合（L10 #2 — `docker-compose.prod.yml` 改為 image-first deployment + 13 顆契約測試）**：L10「Pre-built Docker Images on GHCR (#337)」第 2 顆 checkbox。**做了什麼** —— `docker-compose.prod.yml` 的 `backend` + `frontend` 兩個 service 都加上 `image: ghcr.io/${OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE:-your-org}/omnisight-{backend,frontend}:${OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG:-latest}` 並保留 `build:` 區塊作為 fallback。**為什麼這樣 work** — Docker Compose 官方語意：當同一個 service 同時宣告 `image:` 和 `build:`，`docker compose up` 會先檢查本地 cache → 沒有就嘗試 pull → pull 失敗才 fall back 到 `docker build`，完全 cover「有 registry image 就 pull，沒有才 local build」的需求，**不需要 wrapper script 自己判斷 pull/build 邏輯**。`pull_policy: missing` 顯式設定（雖然是 default），擋未來 drift 到 `always`（每次 up 都強制 pull、慢且依賴網路）或 `build`（永遠不 pull、整個 image-first 契約失效）。**Knobs**（兩個都新增至 `.env.example` 的「Production / Docker Settings」區塊）：(a) `OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE`（lowercase GitHub org/user，default `your-org` placeholder — pull 會 graceful fail 後 build fallback，所以 first-run 不需設定也能跑），(b) `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG`（default `latest`，可 pin `v*` release tag 拿到 reproducible deploys）。Image basename `omnisight-backend` + `omnisight-frontend` 與 `.github/workflows/docker-publish.yml` 的 publishing matrix **完全一致**（被測試 pin 住，rename 一邊另一邊就炸 CI）。**Quick-start.sh 同步調整** — `scripts/quick-start.sh:476` 把 `docker compose ... up -d --build` 改為 `docker compose ... up -d`（`--build` flag 會 force rebuild、繞開 image-first path），diagnostic 加 `GHCR pull 失敗且本地 build 也失敗 (檢查 OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE)`，最後「升級部署」hint 改為兩行：日常用 `pull && up -d`，要強制 build 才用 `--build`。**驗證** `python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(...)"` 解析通過；`docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml config` 用預設 resolve 成 `image: ghcr.io/your-org/omnisight-backend:latest` + `pull_policy: missing` + `build:` 三者並存；`OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE=acme OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG=v1.2.3` 覆寫 resolve 成 `ghcr.io/acme/omnisight-{backend,frontend}:v1.2.3`；`bash -n scripts/quick-start.sh` 乾淨。**測試** 新增 `backend/tests/test_compose_prod_image_first.py`（13 case，mirror 既有 `test_docker_publish_workflow.py` 的 yaml.safe_load + 結構性 assertion 模式，無網路、無 Docker、<200ms 完成）— pin (1) backend+frontend 都宣告 `image:` 指向 `ghcr.io/`、(2) image 字面 contains `${OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE:-your-org}` + `${OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG:-latest}` 防 env-var rename 破壞既有 operator 設定、(3) image basename 必為 `omnisight-{backend,frontend}`、(4) `build:` block 存在 + dockerfile 路徑實存（沒有連 fallback 也炸）、(5) frontend 的 `BACKEND_URL` build arg 保留（fallback build 會 silently 斷掉 prod 前後端 wiring）、(6) `pull_policy: missing` 顯式設定、(7) prometheus + grafana sidecar 維持 pinned version + `observability` profile gate、(8) compose 消費的 image basename **集合相等於** workflow publish 的 image basename — image-first deployment 要求兩邊 agree、(9) `.env.example` 含 `OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE` + `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG` + L10 #337/GHCR 引用、(10) `scripts/quick-start.sh` 的 `docker compose ... up` 行**不能含 `--build`**（會 defeat 契約）— 用 `splitlines()` 過濾 comment / echo 確保只 assert 真實 invocation 行，註解或 user-facing 提示中的 `--build` 不會誤判。**結果** 25 passed in 0.13s（13 新 + 12 既有 publish workflow test 同跑、零 regression）；既有 51 個 `tests/test_quick_start_script.py` 全綠（包含 `test_browser_open_falls_back_gracefully_on_no_desktop` 和全流程 `test_end_to_end_non_interactive_reaches_success_banner` smoke test，pin 住 quick-start 修改沒破壞下游）。**Scope note** — 只做 image-first 切換；multi-arch build / image size 屬後續 L10 子項。**剩餘 L10**：multi-arch build (#3) / image size 優化 (#4) / 效果驗證 (#5)。**Operator 下一步** — 打完 L10 #1 設定的首次 GHCR publish tag 後，在 `.env` 設定 `OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE=<lowercase-org-handle>` 即可享受「pull 30 秒」首次部署。

gerrit-sora/develop:HANDOFF.md:28914:> **上回合（L5 Step 4 #2 — SSE `bootstrap.service.tick` log stream）**：L5「服務啟動 / 健康驗證」第二顆 checkbox — 把 `start-services` 啟動後的 systemd journal / docker compose logs 即時推到 wizard UI，讓 operator 親眼看到服務 boot 過程而不是乾等 `/readyz`。**後端新增**：`backend/routers/bootstrap.py::bootstrap_service_tick`（`GET /api/v1/bootstrap/service-tick`，query `mode` / `compose_file` / `tail=50` / `max_seconds=300`，未登入可用延續 wizard 其它 step 風格，回 `EventSourceResponse`）。**三條 mode 動線**：(a) `systemd` → `journalctl -u omnisight-backend.service -u omnisight-frontend.service --follow --lines <tail> --output short-iso --no-pager`；(b) `docker-compose` → `docker compose -f <file> logs --follow --tail <tail> --no-color`，`compose_file` 空→`docker-compose.prod.yml`；(c) `dev` → short-circuit 不 exec，送一個 `stream=info` 的 tick + `done{reason:dev_noop}` 就關閉（uvicorn / next dev 沒 managed unit 可 tail，不應讓 UI 乾等）。**SSE event 格式**：(1) `start{mode, command[], pid, tail}` 開頭一次；(2) `bootstrap.service.tick{line, stream∈{stdout,stderr,info}, seq, ts}` 每行 log 一顆，`seq` 單調遞增（stdout/stderr 共用序號避免 UI render order race）；(3) `heartbeat{ts}` 每 10s 靜默時推一次避免 proxy 斷線；(4) `done{mode, reason∈{eof,client_disconnect,max_seconds,dev_noop,launcher_missing}, returncode, lines}` 關閉原因明確；(5) `error{detail, mode, command}` 只在 launcher binary 不存在時出現後接 `done`。**Concurrency 設計**：stdout/stderr 各起一條 `_drain_stream` task 把 byte line decode+strip 後丟進 `asyncio.Queue(maxsize=512)`，主 generator 用 `asyncio.wait_for(queue.get(), timeout=heartbeat)` 拉，timeout 時若兩條 drain 都 done 且 queue 空 → 判 EOF 結束；仍活著則推 heartbeat。**清理契約**：`finally` 區必跑 — cancel 兩條 drain、若 proc 還活著 `terminate()` → `wait_for(wait(), 2s)` → 不回來 `kill()` → 再 2s 不回來 returncode=None；避免 client 斷線後殘留 `journalctl -f` / `docker compose logs -f` 佔住 pipe。**Client disconnect 偵測**：每輪 loop 頂先 `request.is_disconnected()`，真斷線直接 `reason=client_disconnect`；`max_seconds` deadline 用 `time.monotonic()` 起跳 clamp 1s 下限（測試可用 `max_seconds=1` 驗證 cap 路徑）。**測試**：新增 `backend/tests/test_bootstrap_service_tick.py` 10 個案：`test_service_tick_dev_mode_is_noop`（dev 不 exec、只吐 info tick）、`test_service_tick_systemd_streams_stdout_lines`（argv 精確匹配 `journalctl -u omnisight-backend.service -u omnisight-frontend.service --follow --lines 20 --output short-iso --no-pager` + 兩行 stdout → seq 1,2）、`test_service_tick_systemd_interleaves_stderr`（stderr 行 tagged `stream=stderr` 與 stdout 共用單調 seq 空間）、`test_service_tick_docker_compose_default_file` / `_custom_file`（`--tail` 數值跟 query、`compose_file` override）、`test_service_tick_invalid_mode_returns_422`、`test_service_tick_binary_missing_emits_error_event`（`FileNotFoundError` → error event + `reason=launcher_missing`）、`test_service_tick_respects_max_seconds`（`hold_open=True` 模擬 infinite tailer、`max_seconds=1` + `heartbeat=0.2s` → `reason=max_seconds` 在 <5s 內收尾）、`test_tick_command_dev_is_empty` / `_clamps_negative_tail`（純 argv builder unit）。**測試 infra 新增**：`_FakeStream(lines, hold_open)` 模擬 `asyncio.subprocess.StreamReader.readline()`（耗盡後可選 hold 在 asyncio.Event 上直到 `terminate()` 放行模擬長駐 tailer）、`_FakeTickProc` 含 `terminate/kill/wait/pid/returncode`、`_patch_tick_exec` monkeypatch `asyncio.create_subprocess_exec`、`_parse_sse()` 把 wire 格式切回 event list、`_collect_stream(client, url)` 用 `httpx.AsyncClient.stream` 開 SSE 收完關閉；`_reset_sse_app_status` autouse fixture 把 `sse_starlette.sse.AppStatus.should_exit_event` 清回 None（否則第二題會踩「Event bound to a different event loop」— pytest-asyncio 每題給新 loop 但 sse_starlette 的 module-level singleton 只認第一個）。**回歸**：`pytest backend/tests/test_bootstrap_service_tick.py backend/tests/test_bootstrap_start_services.py backend/tests/test_bootstrap.py backend/tests/test_bootstrap_cf_tunnel_skip.py backend/tests/test_bootstrap_finalize.py` **48 passed**（10 新 + 38 既有），無 regression。**不做的事**：`/readyz` 輪詢與 4-gate 並行檢查（backend ready / frontend ready / DB migration up-to-date / CF tunnel connector online）仍屬 L5 後續 2 顆 checkbox；前端 SSE hook 與 UI 綠勾勾亦留給下輪。**TODO.md**：L5 第二顆 checkbox `[ ]` → `[x]`；剩餘 2 顆（`/readyz` 輪詢 / 4-gate UI 即時變綠）待續。**後續**：L5 Step 4 #3 — `/readyz` 輪詢到通過 or timeout 180s（後端呼 `httpx.get("/readyz")` 直到 200，成功後 record `STEP_SMOKE` 前置亦可考慮掛在這一步）。

gerrit-sora/develop:TODO.md:2717:- [x] `docker-compose.prod.yml` 改為 `image:` 優先（有 registry image 就 pull，沒有才 local build） *(done: 將 `docker-compose.prod.yml` 從「無條件 local build」改成 image-first deployment — 兩個 app service 都加上 `image: ghcr.io/${OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE:-your-org}/omnisight-{backend,frontend}:${OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG:-latest}` 並保留 `build:` 區塊作為 fallback。**為什麼這樣 work** — Docker Compose 的官方語意：當同一個 service 同時宣告 `image:` 和 `build:`，`docker compose up` 會先檢查本地 cache，沒有就嘗試 pull，pull 失敗才 fall back 到 `docker build`。這完全 cover「有 registry image 就 pull，沒有才 local build」的需求，**不需要 wrapper script 自己判斷 pull/build 邏輯**。`pull_policy: missing` 顯式設定（雖然是 default），是為了擋未來 drift 到 `always`（每次 up 都強制 pull、慢且依賴網路）或 `build`（永遠不 pull、整個 image-first 契約失效）。**Knobs**（兩個都記錄在 `.env.example` 的「Production / Docker Settings」區塊）：(a) `OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE` — lowercase GitHub org/user handle，default `your-org`（placeholder — pull 會 graceful fail → 自動 build fallback，所以即使沒設也能 first-run），(b) `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG` — image tag，default `latest`，可 pin 到 `v*` release tag 拿到 reproducible deploys。Image basename `omnisight-backend` + `omnisight-frontend` **與 `.github/workflows/docker-publish.yml` 的 publishing matrix 完全一致**（已被測試 pin 住，工作流改名會炸 CI）。**Quick-start.sh 同步調整** — `scripts/quick-start.sh:476` 把 `docker compose ... up -d --build` 改為 `docker compose ... up -d`（`--build` flag 會 force rebuild、繞開 image-first path），diagnostic 加一行 `GHCR pull 失敗且本地 build 也失敗 (檢查 OMNISIGHT_GHCR_NAMESPACE)`，最後的「升級部署」hint 也從單行改成兩行：日常用 `pull && up -d`，明確要強制 build 才用 `--build`。**驗證** `python3 -c "import yaml; store `<repo>@sha256:<hex>` 完整 RepoDigest 可能跨 registry 變動（同 image 在 ghcr.io vs docker.io 的 RepoDigest prefix 不同），但 sha256 hex 是 content-addressable、跨 registry 必相同；`_match_digest` (docker_pull.py:353) `endswith("sha256:" + expected)` 邏輯反映此 invariant；test #5 直接用 `f"ghcr.io/vendor/image@sha256:{other_sha}"` 餵入確認非匹配路徑 surface `sha256_layer1_mismatch` 而非 false-positive 把 prefix 當作 mismatch。**設計決策（為何 env scrub test 用真實 bash subprocess vs mock）**：替代 = monkeypatch `subprocess.Popen` + assert env kwarg 不含 OMNISIGHT_INSTALLER_TOKEN；問題：(a) `_scrubbed_env` 邏輯 + `run_in_process_group` 的 env 傳遞鏈是兩個獨立 unit、純 unit-mock 只測一個;  (b) 真 bash + `env > file` 是端到端驗證子進程實際看到的 environment，是 threat model §4.5 想保證的 invariant 級別測試；(c) bash `env` builtin + `>` redirect 不需 vendor 工具鍵、jsdom 等 fragile mock — 在 Linux test runner 上是 zero-dependency 操作；trade-off 為加 ~20ms 跑時、測試強度大幅提升。**Module-global state 稽核**（per `docs/sop/implement_phase_step.md` Step 1）：tests 為 stateless function — `_VALID_SHA` 為 module-level immutable const (sha256 of static byte string)；無 in-memory cache / singleton / thread-locals。`monkeypatch` fixture scope 為 function-level、teardown 自動 unwind subprocess + env mock。Autouse `_isolate_installer_env` fixture 已驗證 `OMNISIGHT_INSTALLER_*` env 不跨測試泄漏 (test #10 `test_no_module_level_mutable_state_in_real_methods` 顯式檢查)。Subprocess (test #9 vendor bash) 在 `run_in_process_group` 1.29 (lands in G5 #6, row 1374)」改成「(landed in G5 #6, row 1374, at `.github/workflows/k8s-helm-smoke.yml`)」過去式 + Scope NOT-included 移除 CI smoke bullet。(6) TODO row 1374 `[ ]`→`[x]` + 本 historical note；(7) HANDOFF 新條目。**為什麼三 job 不合一**：helm-render 失敗立刻紅、kubectl-validate 失敗也立刻紅、kind-smoke 失敗才知道是 admission/deprecation 問題；分離的價值是 PR author 看到 helm-render 紅就知道是 chart 結構問題、看到 kind-smoke 紅就知道是 K8s server 端拒絕。三 job 並行平均跑時間 = max(5, 5) + 15 = 20 min worst case，合一 sequential 是 25 min（節省 5 min/PR），但合一就失去 fail-fast diagnostic value。**為什麼 `--dry-run=server` 不 `--dry-run=client`**：client 端只走 OpenAPI schema 不走 admission webhook，捕不到「v1.29 admission policy 拒絕某 field」這類深層問題；server 走 K8s API 真實 admission chain，是「render + apply cleanly」承諾的唯一誠實實現。**為什麼不真的 apply**：placeholder image `ghcr.io/your-org/omnisight-backend:latest` pull 不到，真的 apply 會 ImagePullBackOff 卡 wait timeout；server-dry-run 既驗證 manifest 又不需要真 image — 真實 deploy 的 readiness 是 G3 blue-green smoke 的事，G5 #6 是 manifest 層的 contract。**為什麼 Gateway-API toggle 用第四次 render 而非第二個 cluster**：toggle 是 chart template 層 `if/else` 互斥 `Ingress` vs `HTTPRoute`，render 階段就決定的事不需要真 cluster；第四次 render + `grep` HTTPRoute 字面是 charter §7.6 explicit toggle 的最便宜 contract。**為什麼 kind cluster 名固定 `omnisight-g5-smoke` 不隨機**：固定名讓 `kind export logs` 命令在 failure step 可預測寫；workflow concurrency.group 已 per-ref cancel-in-progress 不會兩 run 撞同名 cluster。**為什麼 contract test 跨界檢查 G6 dr_drill + G7 grafana**：G5 #6 是 G5 bucket 的 closure row，下一 bucket 是 G6 / G7；本 commit landed CI workflow 後若有人「順手」也加 G6 dr_drill 或 G7 grafana 字面，即 silent 偷跑下一 bucket scope — guard 強制下次 G6 / G7 commit 必須同步 flip 對應 row + 刪 assert（顯性遷移 pattern 從 G5 #3→#4→#5→#6 延續至 G5→G6/G7）。**為什麼 README 不寫「CI 跑了所以 local diff 不必要」**：local diff 是 operator 在 push 前 self-check 的工具、CI 是 author push 後的 enforcement —— 兩者互補不替代；保留 local 命令給 staging operator 在 internal cluster 比 prod 即將 apply 的 manifest。**為什麼三 README 都改 + 加 NOT-included G6/G7 bullet**：G5 是 G5 bucket 的 closure（最後一 row），future reader 看 README 應該一眼看到「G5 全 closed, G6/G7 是下一 bucket」 — 不放 G6/G7 bullet, 5 年後 reader 不知道 G5 後面是什麼會 silent 重 design 已 decided 的 scope。**為什麼跨 helm-render+kind-smoke 鎖 staging/prod overlay 出現次數 ≥2**：render 端綠不代表 server 端綠（client OpenAPI schema 落後 K8s admission policy 2-3 個 minor），必須兩端各跑一次才完整覆蓋；contract `text.count(...) >= 2` 是「render-only sin」的反 regression 鎖。**回歸全綠** — `test_ci_k8s_helm_smoke_g5_6` 50 + `test_helm_chart_g5_5` 121 + `test_k8s_probes_g5_4` 42 + `test_k8s_pdb_g5_3` 42 + `test_k8s_manifests_g5_2` 92 + `test_orchestration_selection_decision` 62 合計 **G5 全 bucket 407 顆全綠 0.76 s 零回歸** + 擴大回歸 `test_healthz_readyz` 21 + `test_blue_green_runbook` 107 + `test_db_failover_runbook` 117 + `test_pg_ha_deployment` 114 = **766 顆全綠 4.93 s 零回歸**。**G5 bucket 全 closure** — rows 1369–1374（charter / manifests / PDB / probes / Helm chart / CI smoke）六顆全 [x]、兩 surfaces（plain manifest + Helm chart）一 truth、CI smoke 把 truth 對 K8s 1.29 round-trip 上鎖。下一可開：G6 row 1378（每日排程：備份 → 另一主機 restore → backup_selftest.py + smoke 子集 → 報告）— G5 全 closed 後 G6/G7 互不相依可平行開。 -->
