   455	    if isinstance(raw_bundle_id, str) and raw_bundle_id:
   456	        bundle_id = raw_bundle_id
   457	    else:
   458	        bundle_id = DEV_FALLBACK_BUNDLE_ID
   459	        if warning is None:
   460	            warning = _NO_BUNDLE_ID_WARNING
   461	
   462	    payload: dict = {
   463	        # Legacy fields preserved for backwards compatibility with the
   464	        # existing v1 frontend bootstrap check.
   465	        "supported_versions": list(SUPPORTED_API_VERSIONS),
   466	        "default_version": DEFAULT_API_VERSION,
   467	        "min_frontend_api_version": MIN_FRONTEND_API_VERSION,
   468	        "deprecated_versions": DEPRECATED_API_VERSIONS,
   469	        # OP-1479 / OP-1491 bundle-aware additions. Keys match the
   470	        # contract shape documented in omnisight-bundle.schema.json so
   471	        # operators can diff /api/version against the bundle artifact
   472	        # directly.
   473	        "bundle_id": bundle_id,
   474	        "api_required": api_required,
   475	        "api_supported": list(api_supported),
   476	        "openapi_hash": openapi_hash,
   477	        "db_migration_head": db_migration_head,
   478	        "frontend_built_against_api": frontend_built_against_api,
   479	        "backend_image_digest": _bundle_image_digest(bundle, "backend"),
   480	        "frontend_image_digest": _bundle_image_digest(bundle, "frontend"),
   481	    }
   482	
   483	    # OP-1582 (RT-08) — deployment overlay. The six identity fields are
   484	    # always present (None when no valid lock was read at startup) so the
   485	    # contract shape is stable across dev images and promoted deployments.
   486	    # ``deploy_overlay_present`` lets a consumer distinguish "dev image, no
   487	    # lock" from "lock read" without inspecting every field.
   488	    overlay_fields = overlay if isinstance(overlay, dict) else {}
   489	    for field in OVERLAY_FIELDS:
   490	        payload[field] = overlay_fields.get(field)
   491	    payload["deploy_overlay_present"] = isinstance(overlay, dict)
   492	
   493	    if warning is not None:
   494	        payload["warning"] = warning
   495	    return payload
   496	
   497	
   498	def install_version_metadata_endpoint(app: FastAPI) -> None:
   499	    """Mount the version-agnostic ``/api/version`` metadata endpoint.
   500	
   501	    OP-1491 — the bundle manifest is read once here at install/startup
   502	    time and cached in a closure (the "module-level state" the ticket
   503	    asks for: a single payload built once, served on every request).
   504	    /api/version sits on the bootstrap-exempt fast path, so we want to
   505	    avoid disk I/O per request. ``build_version_payload`` swallows
   506	    missing/malformed bundle files and resolves to the dev fallback so
   507	    startup never crashes on an image that wasn't built with V1c bake.
   508	
   509	    OP-1582 (RT-08) — the deploy overlay env lock is also read here, once,
   510	    at install/startup time (``init_deploy_overlay``). The cached snapshot
   511	    feeds both the ``/api/version`` payload below and the ``/readyz`` overlay
   512	    gate, so both surfaces serve the identical read-before-start identity.
   513	    """
   514	    init_deploy_overlay()
   515	    cached_payload = build_version_payload()
   516	
   517	    @app.get("/api/version", tags=["api-version"], include_in_schema=False)
   518	    async def _api_version() -> dict:
   519	        return cached_payload

   223	def _atomic_write_text(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
   224	    fd, tmp_name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=str(path.parent))
   225	    try:
   226	        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   227	            fh.write(text)
   228	        os.replace(tmp_name, path)
   229	    finally:
   230	        try:
   231	            os.unlink(tmp_name)
   232	        except FileNotFoundError:
   233	            pass

   235	
   236	class CanaryController:
   237	    """Stage controller for 5% -> 25% -> 100% canary rollout."""
   238	
   239	    def __init__(
   240	        self,
   241	        *,
   242	        writer: CaddyWeightWriter | None = None,
   243	        monitor: SloMonitor | None = None,
   244	        thresholds: SloThresholds | None = None,
   245	        clock: Callable[[], float] = time.time,
   246	    ) -> None:
   247	        self.writer = writer or CaddyWeightWriter()
   248	        self.monitor = monitor or RollingDeploySloMonitor()
   249	        self.thresholds = thresholds or SloThresholds()
   250	        self.clock = clock
   251	
   252	    def start(
   253	        self,
   254	        *,
   255	        rollout_id: str,
   256	        stable_color: str,
   257	        canary_color: str,
   258	    ) -> CanaryState:
   259	        stable = _validate_color(stable_color)
   260	        canary = _validate_color(canary_color)
   261	        if stable == canary:
   262	            raise ValueError("stable_color and canary_color must differ")
   263	        now = self.clock()
   264	        state = CanaryState(
   265	            rollout_id=rollout_id,
   266	            status="running",
   267	            stable_color=stable,
   268	            canary_color=canary,
   269	            stage_index=0,
   270	            stage_started_at=now,
   271	            updated_at=now,
   272	        )
   273	        self.writer.write(state)
   274	        return state
   275	
   276	    def evaluate(self, state: CanaryState) -> tuple[CanaryState, CanaryDecision]:
   277	        if state.status != "running":
   278	            decision = CanaryDecision(
   279	                action=state.status,
   280	                stage=state.stage.name,
   281	                reason=f"rollout is {state.status}",
   282	            )
   283	            return state, decision
   284	
   285	        snapshot = self.monitor.snapshot()
   286	        breach_reason = self._breach_reason(snapshot)
   287	        if breach_reason:
   288	            aborted = self._replace(
   289	                state,
   290	                status="aborted",
   291	                updated_at=self.clock(),
   292	                reason=breach_reason,
   293	            )
   294	            self.writer.write(aborted)
   295	            return aborted, CanaryDecision(
   296	                action="abort",
   297	                stage=state.stage.name,
   298	                reason=breach_reason,
   299	                snapshot=snapshot,
   300	            )
   301	
   302	        now = self.clock()
   303	        if now - state.stage_started_at < state.stage.observe_seconds:
   304	            return state, CanaryDecision(
   305	                action="hold",
   306	                stage=state.stage.name,
   307	                reason="observe_window_open",
   308	                snapshot=snapshot,
   309	            )
   310	
   311	        if state.stage_index == len(CANARY_STAGES) - 1:
   312	            completed = self._replace(
   313	                state,
   314	                status="completed",
   315	                updated_at=now,
   316	                reason="canary_complete",
   317	            )
   318	            self.writer.write(completed)
   319	            return completed, CanaryDecision(
   320	                action="complete",
   321	                stage=state.stage.name,
   322	                reason="canary_complete",
   323	                snapshot=snapshot,
   324	            )
   325	
   326	        advanced = self._replace(
   327	            state,
   328	            stage_index=state.stage_index + 1,
   329	            stage_started_at=now,
   330	            updated_at=now,
   331	            reason="slo_passed",
   332	        )
   333	        self.writer.write(advanced)
   334	        return advanced, CanaryDecision(
   335	            action="advance",
   336	            stage=advanced.stage.name,
   337	            reason="slo_passed",
   338	            snapshot=snapshot,
   339	        )
   340	
   341	    def manual_control(
   342	        self,
   343	        state: CanaryState,
   344	        command: str,
   345	        *,
   346	        reason: str = "operator",
   347	    ) -> CanaryState:
   348	        now = self.clock()
   349	        if command == "pause":
   350	            new_state = self._replace(
   351	                state, status="paused", updated_at=now, reason=reason
   352	            )
   353	        elif command == "resume":
   354	            new_state = self._replace(
   355	                state, status="running", stage_started_at=now,
   356	                updated_at=now, reason=reason,
   357	            )
   358	        elif command == "advance":
   359	            next_index = min(state.stage_index + 1, len(CANARY_STAGES) - 1)
   360	            new_state = self._replace(
   361	                state,
   362	                status="running",
   363	                stage_index=next_index,
   364	                stage_started_at=now,
   365	                updated_at=now,
   366	                reason=reason,
   367	            )
   368	        elif command == "abort":
   369	            new_state = self._replace(
   370	                state, status="aborted", updated_at=now, reason=reason
   371	            )
   372	        else:
   373	            raise ValueError("command must be pause, resume, advance, or abort")
   374	        self.writer.write(new_state)
   375	        return new_state
   376	
   377	    def _breach_reason(self, snapshot: SloSnapshot) -> str:
   378	        if snapshot.error_rate >= self.thresholds.max_error_rate:
   379	            return f"slo_error_rate:{snapshot.error_rate:.4f}"
   380	        if snapshot.p95_latency_ms > self.thresholds.max_p95_latency_ms:
   800	        return "canary" if self.canary_active() else "full"
   801	
   802	    def trigger(self, reason: str) -> RollbackOutcome:
   803	        if self.canary_active():
   804	            return self.canary_trigger(reason)
   805	        return self.full_trigger(reason)
   806	
   807	
   808	def build_default_rollback(
   809	    *, alert: Callable[[str, str], None] | None = None,
   810	    migration_safety_probe: RollbackMigrationSafetyProbe | None = None,
   811	) -> RollbackTrigger:
   812	    """Wire D10 canary abort + D9 full prod rollback.
   813	
   814	    The canary side defers to :mod:`backend.canary_rollout` (its
   815	    ``manual_control(state, "abort")``); the full side defers to
   816	    :mod:`backend.production_release`. Both are kept inside the closure
   817	    so importing this module from tests does not eagerly load the
   818	    deploy plumbing.
   819	    """
   820	
   821	    def _canary_active() -> bool:
   822	        from backend import canary_rollout as cr
   823	        try:
   824	            state = cr.load_state()
   825	        except FileNotFoundError:
   826	            return False
   827	        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover -- corrupt state -> no canary
   828	            logger.exception("canary state load failed; assuming no canary")
   829	            return False
   830	        return state.status in {"running", "paused"}
   831	
   832	    def _canary_trigger(reason: str) -> RollbackOutcome:
   833	        from backend import canary_rollout as cr
   834	        state = cr.load_state()
   835	        controller = cr.CanaryController()
   836	        new_state = controller.manual_control(
   837	            state, "abort", reason=reason,
   838	        )
   839	        return RollbackOutcome(
   840	            mode="canary",
   841	            status="aborted",
   842	            detail=f"rollout_id={new_state.rollout_id} status={new_state.status}",
   843	        )
   844	
   845	    def _full_rollback_action(tag: str) -> None:
   846	        from backend import production_release
   847	        orch = production_release.ProductionDeployOrchestrator()
   848	        orch._rollback(tag)  # noqa: SLF001 -- intentional: D9 hook
   849	
   850	    full_executor = ComposeRollbackExecutor(

     1	# Canary Rollout Runbook — D10 (OP-882)
     2	
     3	**Owner:** Deploy on-call · **Spec:** META OP-761 §Phase 3 · **Ticket:** OP-882
     4	
     5	This runbook is the procedure for shifting production traffic onto a
     6	new image via the staged 5% → 25% → 100% canary controlled by
     7	`backend.orchestrator.canary.CanaryOrchestrator`. It is invoked
     8	*after* the D9 prod-deploy orchestrator (`backend/orchestrator/prod_deploy.py`)
     9	has placed the new image behind the canary upstream — D10 only governs
    10	the traffic shift.
    11	
    12	## 0. Pre-flight
    13	
    14	| Check | Command / location |
    15	| --- | --- |
    16	| D9 prod-deploy succeeded for the release | `prod_deploy_audit.status='completed'` for the release_id |
    17	| Canary upstream is live and `/readyz` 200 | `curl -sf $OMNISIGHT_CANARY_UPSTREAM/readyz` |
    18	| Stable allocation is currently 100% of traffic | `head -2 deploy/caddy/canary.caddy` shows `canary-stage: rollback` or stage `100` of a prior rollout |
    19	| Baseline p95 latency captured | Note the value used for `SloBaseline(p95_latency_ms=…)` — usually the 7-day rolling median from the metric-baseline comparator (`backend/staging_validation.py`) |
    20	| Operator paged in `#deploy-canary` Slack | Manual gates require an attending operator |
    21	
    22	## 1. Stages and SLO bounds
    23	
    24	| Stage | Canary % | Observe window | Manual gate |
    25	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
    26	| `5` | 5 | 5 minutes | Yes |
    27	| `25` | 25 | 10 minutes | Yes |
    28	| `100` | 100 | n/a (terminal) | Yes — completes the rollout |
    29	
    30	The SLO gate at each transition enforces:
    31	
    32	* `error_rate < 0.5%` over the observe window
    33	* p95 latency within **±20%** of the captured baseline
    34	
    35	A breach on either signal is a **CanaryGateFailed** — see §3.
    36	
    37	## 2. Happy-path procedure
    38	
    39	1. **Start the rollout.** Call:
    40	
    41	       orch = CanaryOrchestrator(
    42	           rollout_id="<release_id>",
    43	           stable_color="blue",
    44	           canary_color="green",
    45	           baseline=SloBaseline(p95_latency_ms=<baseline_ms>),
    46	       )
    47	       orch.start()
    48	
    49	   The writer renders `deploy/caddy/canary.caddy` with
    50	   `lb_policy weighted_round_robin 95 5`. Trigger `caddy reload` (or
    51	   `systemctl reload caddy` on production). An SSE event
    52	   `canary.stage.started` fires with `stage="5"`.
    53	
    54	2. **Observe for 5 min.** Watch the `canary.stage.*` SSE feed and the
    55	   Grafana panel `omnisight-canary-rollout`. Do **not** call
    56	   `orch.advance()` before the observe window closes — the manual gate
    57	   will reject with `CanaryGateFailed: observe window open`.
    58	
    59	3. **Advance to 25%.** Call `orch.advance()`. The orchestrator pulls
    60	   a fresh SLO snapshot, verifies the bounds, rewrites
    61	   `deploy/caddy/canary.caddy` with `lb_policy weighted_round_robin
    62	   75 25`, and emits `canary.stage.transitioned` with
    63	   `from_stage="5"`. Reload Caddy.
    64	
    65	4. **Observe for 10 min**, then `orch.advance()` again. The 100%
    66	   snippet (`weighted_round_robin 0 100`) is rendered and
    67	   `canary.stage.transitioned` fires with `from_stage="25"`.
    68	
    69	5. **Promote to terminal.** One final `orch.advance()` flips the
    70	   internal state to `completed` and emits `canary.completed`. No
    71	   snippet rewrite is needed since 100% is already in place.
    72	
    73	## 3. Failure modes and rollback
    74	
    75	### 3.1 `CanaryGateFailed` — SLO breach
    76	
    77	The orchestrator has *already* emitted `canary.gate.failed` (carrying
    78	the offending snapshot) and triggered `rollback(reason=…)` which
    79	restores the 100% stable allocation by writing
    80	`lb_policy weighted_round_robin 100 0`. Trigger `caddy reload`. The

codex

     1	# Deploy-line deep audit — dev → testing → staging → canary → prod (2026-05-25)
     2	
     3	3-way audit (me/live-state + 2 sub-agents + codex), cross-verified against live state, ADR-0040/0042,
     4	the 2026-05-22 release-train readiness handoff, and the prod-deploy runbook. Sources reconciled; two
     5	sub-agent findings were corrected by live evidence (noted). Codex review:
     6	`docs/audit/codex-reviews/deploy-pipeline-audit-codex-2026-05-25.txt`.
     7	
     8	## Verdict
     9	The **happy path works** — we shipped v0.6.0 end-to-end today (candidate CI → staging → canary → promote
    10	→ prod), and the core safety primitives are real + verified (env-contract guard, fail-closed-on-absent
    11	overlay, promote-time digest binding, candidate signing, atomic lock writer). **But the GATING/ENFORCEMENT
    12	layer that's supposed to PROTECT the line is substantially half-inert or wrong, ADR-0040/0042 are only
    13	partially enforced, and there are real security leaks.** The line *ships*; it does not fully *protect*.
    14	
    15	## BLOCKER
    16	1. **[COSIGN/SEC] CI cosign sign+attest omit `--tlog-upload=false`** → candidate image digests + metadata
    17	   leak to the public Sigstore Rekor log. The v0.6.0 candidate signing today leaked. `.gitlab-ci.yml:404,466,497,550`
    18	   (0 `tlog-upload=false`). Promote (`sign_promotion_attestation.py`) is correct; only CI build-signing leaks.
    19	   Violates handoff §6/§55. (Recurrence of the v0.5.0 Rekor-leak lesson.)
    20	2. **[ADR/SEC] GHCR not decommissioned** — `.github/workflows/build-images.yml` (REGISTRY=ghcr.io, on push+`v*`+cron)
    21	   + `docker-publish.yml` are `state=active`. **The OP-1687 mirror restore today re-triggered them** (Build Images /
    22	   Docker Publish / Release fired on the 14:41Z push, all failed). Latent: fixing GHCR creds resumes GHCR pushes +
    23	   `v*`-tag builds (RT-20 divergent-digest hazard). Violates ADR-0042:20-21, ADR-0040:34.
    24	3. **[STAGING] `/readyz` overlay gate checks lock SHAPE, not SUBSTANCE** — `_check_deploy_overlay` (health.py:481-490)
    25	   returns true whenever the lock exists + is well-formed; never cross-checks lock digests vs the *running* image.
    26	   A stale/hand-edited well-formed lock passes /readyz + the staging gate. (Digest binding exists only at promote,
    27	   `assert_staging_gate_passed`.)
    28	4. **[CANARY] smoke gate permanently dead** — `staging-gate-smoke.timer` not installed (only `.service` linked);
    29	   last smoke record RED (401 no-credential), wrong revision (`fa862a1`), >2 days stale. R3 smoke gate can never green.
    30	5. **[PROMOTE] `promote_image_bundle.py` default registry is WRONG** — `DEFAULT_REGISTRY = sora.services:49154/omnisight/OmniSight-Productizer`
    31	   (web port + wrong case) vs the real `:49160/omnisight/omnisight-productizer`. Omitting `--registry` promotes to the
    32	   wrong CR namespace. (promote_image_bundle.py:51.) Violates ADR-0042/handoff §12.
    33	6. **[PROD] `deploy-prod.sh --digest` path broken** — writes `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_DIGEST` then sets `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG`
    34	   to the digest; compose ignores `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_DIGEST` + uses tag syntax → invalid ref or deploys old tag.
    35	   (deploy-prod.sh:255-278 vs docker-compose.prod.yml:152.) The advertised "deploy by digest" (ADR-0040:36/82) is unwired.
    36	7. **[DEV] A1 `REVOKE CONNECT` not implemented** — the "only provable" DB-level dev→prod prevention exists only in the
    37	   dev-compose comment + env_contract docstring; no migration/script applies it. App-level A2 guard works; DB-level
    38	   belt-and-suspenders does not. Violates the A1 design doc.
    39	
    40	## HIGH
    41	8. **[PROD/B] prod-deploy orchestrator (path B) is multiply broken** (OP-1690 + new): router unmounted (dead API,
    42	   main.py never includes `prod_deploy.router`); Slack gate = env-allowlist stub; smoke target = nonexistent

**Findings**

BLOCKER — coverage — finding #13 is missing from the plan. The coverage map claims all 35, but #13 “staging `.env` `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG=latest`” is not mapped to any ticket. Evidence: draft coverage map omits #13 at `docs/architecture/2026-05-25-deploy-pipeline-remediation-draft.md:145`; audit finding is at `docs/audit/2026-05-25-deploy-pipeline-deep-audit.md:54`.

BLOCKER — B1 — the proposed “running image digest” source is not honest yet. Current backend only reads the overlay lock and returns true if present; there is no independent runtime source. Evidence: `_check_deploy_overlay` returns true on any cached overlay at `backend/routers/health.py:477-487`; the overlay is only parsed from `/etc/omnisight/deploy-overlay.lock` at `backend/api_versioning.py:336-369`. Reading `/app/bundle.json` is not sufficient because `/api/version` already emits placeholder digests from that bundle path at `backend/api_versioning.py:479-480`, matching audit #23. Required change: B1 must add deploy/runtime wiring for an independent source, not only backend code. The honest options are host/orchestrator injection from Docker inspect at container start, or a constrained Docker API self-inspect path. The bundle is not honest for this check.

BLOCKER — C2 — `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_DIGEST` as a single variable cannot correctly pin the prod compose pair. Prod has separate backend and frontend images at `docker-compose.prod.yml:152` and `docker-compose.prod.yml:416`; promote tracks backend+frontend as a pair, not one digest (`scripts/promote_image_bundle.py:56-58`). A single digest cannot identify both images. Compose can deploy digest refs, but the ticket should switch to full image-ref variables or per-image suffixes, e.g. `OMNISIGHT_BACKEND_IMAGE_REF=.../backend@sha256:...` and `OMNISIGHT_FRONTEND_IMAGE_REF=.../frontend@sha256:...`. The current draft’s “support `@${OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_DIGEST}`” is under-specified and likely wrong. Evidence: broken script writes one digest at `scripts/deploy-prod.sh:255-264`, then still upserts `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG` at `scripts/deploy-prod.sh:278-283`.

BLOCKER — D1 — `REVOKE CONNECT ... FROM <dev role>` alone may not block the dev role if `PUBLIC` still has database CONNECT. PostgreSQL grants CONNECT to PUBLIC by default, so the safe script must account for PUBLIC and explicitly grant required roles back, e.g. revoke PUBLIC, grant prod app/operator roles, verify `has_database_privilege`. The draft only says revoke from dev role and “must not revoke prod app role” at `docs/architecture/2026-05-25-deploy-pipeline-remediation-draft.md:74-77`; that is not enough to implement the audit’s DB-level belt-and-suspenders requirement at `docs/audit/2026-05-25-deploy-pipeline-deep-audit.md:36-38`.

SHOULD-FIX — A2 — retiring GHCR workflows has hidden repo test/doc dependents. It will break GHCR contract tests unless the ticket explicitly rewrites/removes them. Evidence: tests directly assert `docker-publish.yml`, GHCR, and image names (`backend/tests/test_docker_publish_workflow.py`, `backend/tests/test_compose_prod_image_first.py`, `backend/tests/test_pull_30s_effect.py`, `backend/tests/test_build_images_pipeline.py`) from grep output; the workflow itself is GHCR-only at `.github/workflows/docker-publish.yml:35` and build-images is GHCR-only at `.github/workflows/build-images.yml:58`. Answer to open Q2: remove `docker-publish.yml`; do not re-point it. Its non-GHCR consumer is mostly stale tests/docs, not a live deploy path. Update those in A2 or split a dependent test-retirement ticket.

SHOULD-FIX — A2 — order/dependency should say A1 is a hard block before merging A2. The draft mentions `A1→A2` only in the coverage footer at line 147, but A2 edits the mirrored repo content that can itself trigger active GitHub workflows until A1 is complete. Make it an explicit `Blocks: A1` on A2. Evidence: active push triggers are in audit lines `20-23`; A1 disables mirror workflows at draft lines `15-19`.

SHOULD-FIX — B3 — “remove `orchestrator/canary.py`” is too strong. It is not live-mounted, but it is still referenced by tests/docs/ADR and canary template comments. Evidence: live router uses `backend.canary_rollout` at `backend/main.py:1556-1557`; runbook still instructs `backend.orchestrator.canary.CanaryOrchestrator` at `docs/operations/canary-runbook.md:5-8`; `_StubMonitor` default is at `backend/orchestrator/canary.py:121-125` and `:178`. Killing the module will break tests and historical docs. Prefer deprecate/test-only mark plus runbook switch, then later delete with a dedicated cleanup.

SHOULD-FIX — B3/F3 — F3 must not touch `deploy/blue-green` until canary paths are moved or retired. The draft says delete/mark `deploy/{k8s,helm,render,railway,digitalocean,blue-green}` at `docs/architecture/...:131-133`, but live `backend.canary_rollout` defaults state/snippet paths into `deploy/blue-green` at `backend/canary_rollout.py:22-25`. That is a live canary dependency, not dead deploy-target clutter.

SHOULD-FIX — D4 — prod `.env` is operator state, not an agent-auto repo edit. The repo can remove `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_PATH` from compose and require `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`, but applying the prod `.env` change is tier:X. Evidence: compose injects SQLite path in both backend replicas at `docker-compose.prod.yml:174` and `:303`; audit says live prod uses PG and SQLite is only a latent fallback at `docs/audit/...:69-71`. Answer to D4: SQLite remains legitimate for dev/tests/legacy local paths, not prod.

SHOULD-FIX — owner classification — several `agent:auto` tickets touch prod/security boundaries and should be tier:X or split into agent patch + operator apply/review: B1 health gate can 503 staging/prod; C2 prod deploy image refs can break live deploy; D2/D3 alter the DB env-contract security guard; E3 touches config validation and can loosen prod runtime validation. Evidence: B1 draft lines `37-42`, C2 lines `63-66`, D2/D3 lines `79-85`, E3 lines `110-113`. A2 is also CI/release automation; safe for an agent to patch only after A1, but should be tier:X-reviewed before merge.

NICE — E3 — the “ignore unknown env” direction has over-build risk. `backend/config.py` has one global `Settings` model with no scoped tooling model at `backend/config.py:10`; making it globally permissive would violate the ticket’s own MUST-NOT. Prefer a release-tooling settings wrapper or scoped sanitized env loader.

**Open Questions**

1. B1 honest source: not `/app/bundle.json`. Use deploy/orchestrator-provided per-image digest env generated from Docker/registry inspect at the same moment the container image ref is selected, or a constrained Docker API self-inspect. Current backend has no honest source.
2. A2: remove `docker-publish.yml`; no live non-GHCR consumer found. The consumers are stale tests/docs that must be updated.
3. D5: fix the docs/claims and bind localhost. Do not add frontend/caddy/backend-b unless dev genuinely needs staging parity. Current dev compose is intentionally minimal/inert at `deploy/dev/docker-compose.yml:1-9` and only defines postgres/backend-a/dag-executor.
4. Mis-grouping/ownership: #13 is missing. B1/C2/D2/D3/E3 should be tier:X or split with operator-owned apply/review.
5. C3/OP-1690: retire path B. Evidence favors deprecation: router unmounted, Slack stub, bad smoke default, and wrong env var per audit lines `41-44`; live path shipped v0.6.0 without it.

**Verdict**

NEEDS-REVISION.

Required changes:
1. Add a ticket for finding #13.
2. Rewrite B1 around an independent runtime digest source and include deploy wiring.
3. Rewrite C2 to use per-image digest/full-ref variables, not one `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_DIGEST`.
4. Fix D1 SQL scope for PUBLIC/prod-role grants and verification.
5. Add A2 dependent test/doc cleanup and make A1 a hard block.
6. Tighten B3/F3 so live canary paths are not deleted accidentally.
7. Reclassify B1/C2/D2/D3/E3, and probably A2, as tier:X-reviewed or split operator-owned apply steps.

Evidence: tests directly assert `docker-publish.yml`, GHCR, and image names (`backend/tests/test_docker_publish_workflow.py`, `backend/tests/test_compose_prod_image_first.py`, `backend/tests/test_pull_30s_effect.py`, `backend/tests/test_build_images_pipeline.py`) from grep output; the workflow itself is GHCR-only at `.github/workflows/docker-publish.yml:35` and build-images is GHCR-only at `.github/workflows/build-images.yml:58`. Answer to open Q2: remove `docker-publish.yml`; do not re-point it. Its non-GHCR consumer is mostly stale tests/docs, not a live deploy path. Update those in A2 or split a dependent test-retirement ticket.

SHOULD-FIX — A2 — order/dependency should say A1 is a hard block before merging A2. The draft mentions `A1→A2` only in the coverage footer at line 147, but A2 edits the mirrored repo content that can itself trigger active GitHub workflows until A1 is complete. Make it an explicit `Blocks: A1` on A2. Evidence: active push triggers are in audit lines `20-23`; A1 disables mirror workflows at draft lines `15-19`.

SHOULD-FIX — B3 — “remove `orchestrator/canary.py`” is too strong. It is not live-mounted, but it is still referenced by tests/docs/ADR and canary template comments. Evidence: live router uses `backend.canary_rollout` at `backend/main.py:1556-1557`; runbook still instructs `backend.orchestrator.canary.CanaryOrchestrator` at `docs/operations/canary-runbook.md:5-8`; `_StubMonitor` default is at `backend/orchestrator/canary.py:121-125` and `:178`. Killing the module will break tests and historical docs. Prefer deprecate/test-only mark plus runbook switch, then later delete with a dedicated cleanup.

SHOULD-FIX — B3/F3 — F3 must not touch `deploy/blue-green` until canary paths are moved or retired. The draft says delete/mark `deploy/{k8s,helm,render,railway,digitalocean,blue-green}` at `docs/architecture/...:131-133`, but live `backend.canary_rollout` defaults state/snippet paths into `deploy/blue-green` at `backend/canary_rollout.py:22-25`. That is a live canary dependency, not dead deploy-target clutter.

SHOULD-FIX — D4 — prod `.env` is operator state, not an agent-auto repo edit. The repo can remove `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_PATH` from compose and require `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`, but applying the prod `.env` change is tier:X. Evidence: compose injects SQLite path in both backend replicas at `docker-compose.prod.yml:174` and `:303`; audit says live prod uses PG and SQLite is only a latent fallback at `docs/audit/...:69-71`. Answer to D4: SQLite remains legitimate for dev/tests/legacy local paths, not prod.

SHOULD-FIX — owner classification — several `agent:auto` tickets touch prod/security boundaries and should be tier:X or split into agent patch + operator apply/review: B1 health gate can 503 staging/prod; C2 prod deploy image refs can break live deploy; D2/D3 alter the DB env-contract security guard; E3 touches config validation and can loosen prod runtime validation. Evidence: B1 draft lines `37-42`, C2 lines `63-66`, D2/D3 lines `79-85`, E3 lines `110-113`. A2 is also CI/release automation; safe for an agent to patch only after A1, but should be tier:X-reviewed before merge.

NICE — E3 — the “ignore unknown env” direction has over-build risk. `backend/config.py` has one global `Settings` model with no scoped tooling model at `backend/config.py:10`; making it globally permissive would violate the ticket’s own MUST-NOT. Prefer a release-tooling settings wrapper or scoped sanitized env loader.

**Open Questions**

1. B1 honest source: not `/app/bundle.json`. Use deploy/orchestrator-provided per-image digest env generated from Docker/registry inspect at the same moment the container image ref is selected, or a constrained Docker API self-inspect. Current backend has no honest source.
2. A2: remove `docker-publish.yml`; no live non-GHCR consumer found. The consumers are stale tests/docs that must be updated.
3. D5: fix the docs/claims and bind localhost. Do not add frontend/caddy/backend-b unless dev genuinely needs staging parity. Current dev compose is intentionally minimal/inert at `deploy/dev/docker-compose.yml:1-9` and only defines postgres/backend-a/dag-executor.
4. Mis-grouping/ownership: #13 is missing. B1/C2/D2/D3/E3 should be tier:X or split with operator-owned apply/review.
5. C3/OP-1690: retire path B. Evidence favors deprecation: router unmounted, Slack stub, bad smoke default, and wrong env var per audit lines `41-44`; live path shipped v0.6.0 without it.

**Verdict**

NEEDS-REVISION.

Required changes:
1. Add a ticket for finding #13.
2. Rewrite B1 around an independent runtime digest source and include deploy wiring.
3. Rewrite C2 to use per-image digest/full-ref variables, not one `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_DIGEST`.
4. Fix D1 SQL scope for PUBLIC/prod-role grants and verification.
5. Add A2 dependent test/doc cleanup and make A1 a hard block.
6. Tighten B3/F3 so live canary paths are not deleted accidentally.
7. Reclassify B1/C2/D2/D3/E3, and probably A2, as tier:X-reviewed or split operator-owned apply steps.
